import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass

import pytest
from utils import ServerProcess, download_file

# Runs each speculative method greedily and checks that it still drafts and accepts as expected.
# accept rate = accepted / drafted, accept len = 1 + accepted / verification steps (from /metrics).
# Needs real models and a GPU. Use SPEC_METHODS=compact, nightly, all, or a comma-separated method list.

HF = "https://huggingface.co"
MODEL_REPO = "ggml-org/spec-for-testing-GGUF"
MODEL_REVISION = "a0736c8e98d3fe580352dacffa3fbd5e9c22a437"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SpecCase:
    name: str
    spec_type: str
    target: str
    draft: str | None  # None when the heads are part of the target (MTP)
    n_max: int
    min_accept_rate: float
    min_accept_len: float


CASES = [
    # Compact floors are the minimum of three identical GB10 calibration runs
    # against current master minus six acceptance-rate percentage points and
    # 0.2 accepted tokens per verification step.
    SpecCase(
        name="mtp",
        spec_type="draft-mtp",
        target="Qwen3.5-4B-MTP-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        draft=None,
        n_max=3,
        min_accept_rate=0.6040,
        min_accept_len=2.7766,
    ),
    SpecCase(
        name="dflash",
        spec_type="draft-dflash",
        target="Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        draft="dflash-Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        n_max=7,
        min_accept_rate=0.1404,
        min_accept_len=2.1687,
    ),
    SpecCase(
        name="dspark",
        spec_type="draft-dspark",
        target="Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        draft="dspark-Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        n_max=7,
        min_accept_rate=0.2767,
        min_accept_len=3.1246,
    ),
    SpecCase(
        name="eagle3",
        spec_type="draft-eagle3",
        target="Qwen3-4B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        draft="eagle3-Qwen3-4B-Q8_0.gguf",
        n_max=3,
        min_accept_rate=0.2006,
        min_accept_len=1.5757,
    ),
    # Nightly floors retain the earlier three-point rate and 0.2 length margins.
    SpecCase(
        name="dflash2",
        spec_type="draft-dflash2",
        target="Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_K_M.gguf",
        draft="dflash2-Qwen3.8-27B-Q8_0.gguf",
        n_max=7,
        min_accept_rate=0.5180,
        min_accept_len=4.5414,
    ),
    SpecCase(
        name="lilicorr",
        spec_type="draft-lilicorr",
        target="NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-74B-A9B-24GB.gguf",
        draft="lilicorr-NVIDIA-Nemotron-3.5-Bolt-74B-A9B-24GB-NVFP4.gguf",
        n_max=7,
        min_accept_rate=0.6399,
        min_accept_len=5.4444,
    ),
]

COMPACT_METHODS = {"mtp", "dflash", "dspark", "eagle3"}
NIGHTLY_METHODS = {"dflash2", "lilicorr"}

PROMPTS = [
    "Write a Python function that returns the n-th Fibonacci number using memoization.\n\n```python\n",
    "The three main differences between TCP and UDP are:\n1.",
    "Question: What is the capital of France, and what river flows through it?\nAnswer:",
]

N_PREDICT = 128


def model_path(file: str) -> str:
    """Download a pinned GGUF from the consolidated test repository."""
    model_dir = os.environ.get("SPEC_MODEL_DIR", "./tmp")
    os.makedirs(model_dir, exist_ok=True)
    return download_file(
        f"{HF}/{MODEL_REPO}/resolve/{MODEL_REVISION}/{file}",
        os.path.join(model_dir, file),
    )


def make_server(case: SpecCase) -> ServerProcess:
    server = ServerProcess()
    server.model_hf_repo = None
    server.model_hf_file = None
    server.model_file = model_path(case.target)
    server.n_ctx = 1024
    server.n_slots = 1
    server.n_gpu_layer = int(os.environ.get("N_GPU_LAYERS", 99))
    server.fa = "on"
    server.temperature = 0.0
    server.server_metrics = True
    server.spec_type = case.spec_type
    server.spec_draft_n_gpu_layer = server.n_gpu_layer
    if case.draft:
        server.model_draft = model_path(case.draft)
    if case.n_max:
        server.spec_draft_n_max = case.n_max
    return server


def generate(server: ServerProcess) -> list[list[int]]:
    outputs = []
    for prompt in PROMPTS:
        res = server.make_request(
            "POST",
            "/completion",
            data={
                "prompt": prompt,
                "temperature": 0.0,
                "top_k": 1,
                "seed": 42,
                "n_predict": N_PREDICT,
                "cache_prompt": False,
                "return_tokens": True,
            },
        )
        assert res.status_code == 200
        outputs.append(res.body["tokens"])
    return outputs


def spec_metrics(server: ServerProcess) -> dict[str, float]:
    # Use exact cumulative counters: the equivalent server log values are rounded
    # and emitted separately for each request.
    res = server.make_request("GET", "/metrics")
    assert res.status_code == 200
    values = {}
    for name in ("draft_tokens", "accepted_tokens", "drafts"):
        m = re.search(
            rf"^llamacpp:spec_decode_num_{name}_total (\S+)$", res.body, re.MULTILINE
        )
        assert m is not None, f"missing spec_decode_num_{name}_total in /metrics"
        values[name] = float(m.group(1))
    return values


def selected_cases():
    wanted = os.environ.get("SPEC_METHODS")
    if not wanted:
        return []
    aliases = {
        "all": {case.name for case in CASES},
        "compact": COMPACT_METHODS,
        "nightly": NIGHTLY_METHODS,
    }
    selected = aliases.get(
        wanted, {name.strip() for name in wanted.split(",") if name.strip()}
    )
    unknown = selected - {c.name for c in CASES}
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"unknown SPEC_METHODS: {', '.join(sorted(unknown))}")
    return [case for case in CASES if case.name in selected]


@pytest.mark.heavy
@pytest.mark.slow
@pytest.mark.parametrize("case", selected_cases(), ids=lambda c: c.name)
def test_spec_method(case: SpecCase):
    server = make_server(case)
    try:
        server.start(timeout_seconds=1800)
        outputs = generate(server)
        m = spec_metrics(server)
    finally:
        server.stop()

    accept_rate = m["accepted_tokens"] / m["draft_tokens"] if m["draft_tokens"] else 0.0
    accept_len = 1.0 + m["accepted_tokens"] / m["drafts"] if m["drafts"] else 1.0
    print(
        f"SPEC_RESULT {case.name}: drafted={m['draft_tokens']:.0f} accepted={m['accepted_tokens']:.0f} "
        f"steps={m['drafts']:.0f} accept_rate={accept_rate:.4f} accept_len={accept_len:.4f}"
    )

    assert all(outputs), f"{case.name}: at least one request returned no tokens"
    assert m["draft_tokens"] > 0, f"{case.name}: no tokens were drafted"
    assert m["drafts"] > 0, f"{case.name}: no speculative verification steps ran"
    assert (
        accept_rate >= case.min_accept_rate
    ), f"{case.name}: accept rate {accept_rate:.3f} < {case.min_accept_rate}"
    assert (
        accept_len >= case.min_accept_len
    ), f"{case.name}: accept length {accept_len:.3f} < {case.min_accept_len}"


#include "testing.h"

#include "../src/llama-mmap.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <system_error>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include <malloc.h>
#endif

static constexpr size_t test_file_size = LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE + 3*1024*1024 + 1;
static constexpr size_t test_io_size   = 1024*1024;

static uint8_t test_byte(size_t offset) {
    // Mix high offset bits into each byte to detect repeated chunks.
    uint64_t value = offset;
    value = (value ^ (value >> 30)) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    value = (value ^ (value >> 27)) * 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
    return static_cast<uint8_t>(value ^ (value >> 31));
}

static bool matches(const void * data, size_t offset, size_t size) {
    const auto * bytes = static_cast<const uint8_t *>(data);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
        if (bytes[i] != test_byte(offset + i)) {
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

static void check_test_pattern(testing & t) {
    uint8_t buffer[256];
    for (size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(buffer); ++i) {
        buffer[i] = test_byte(i);
    }
    t.assert_true("reject data repeated after 512 KiB", !matches(buffer, 512 * 1024, sizeof(buffer)));
    t.assert_true("reject data repeated after a direct I/O chunk", !matches(buffer, LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE, sizeof(buffer)));
}

struct temporary_file {
    std::filesystem::path path;

    temporary_file() {
        const auto now = std::chrono::steady_clock::now().time_since_epoch().count();
        path = std::filesystem::temp_directory_path() / ("test-llama-mmap-" + std::to_string(now) + ".bin");
    }

    ~temporary_file() {
        std::error_code error;
        std::filesystem::remove(path, error);
    }
};

struct aligned_buffer {
    void * data = nullptr;

    aligned_buffer(size_t alignment, size_t size) {
        alignment = std::max(alignment, alignof(std::max_align_t));
#if defined(_WIN32)
        data = _aligned_malloc(size, alignment);
#else
        if (posix_memalign(&data, alignment, size) != 0) {
            data = nullptr;
        }
#endif
    }

    ~aligned_buffer() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
        _aligned_free(data);
#else
        free(data);
#endif
    }

    aligned_buffer(const aligned_buffer &) = delete;
    aligned_buffer & operator=(const aligned_buffer &) = delete;
};

static void write_test_file(const std::filesystem::path & path) {
    std::ofstream file(path, std::ios::binary);
    if (!file) {
        throw std::runtime_error("failed to create test file");
    }

    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(test_io_size);
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < test_file_size; offset += buffer.size()) {
        const size_t size = std::min(buffer.size(), test_file_size - offset);
        for (size_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {
            buffer[i] = test_byte(offset + i);
        }
        file.write(reinterpret_cast<const char *>(buffer.data()), size);
        if (!file) {
            throw std::runtime_error("failed to write test file");
        }
    }
}

static void check_file(testing & t, const std::filesystem::path & path, bool use_direct_io) {
    llama_file file(path.string().c_str(), "rb", use_direct_io);
    if (use_direct_io && !file.has_direct_io()) {
        t.skip("direct I/O is unavailable for the test file");
        return;
    }
    t.assert_equal("requested I/O mode", use_direct_io, file.has_direct_io());
    t.assert_equal(test_file_size, file.size());
    t.assert_true("read alignment is nonzero", file.read_alignment() != 0);

    const size_t alignment = file.read_alignment();
    const std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>> ranges = {
        { 0, 1 },
        { 1, 31 },
        { alignment - 1, alignment + 3 },
        { LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE - 11, 27 },
        { test_file_size - 31, 31 },
        { 1, LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE + 31 },
        { 1, test_file_size - 1 },
        {0, test_file_size}
    };
    for (const auto & range : ranges) {
        std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(range.second);
        file.seek(range.first, SEEK_SET);
        file.read_raw(buffer.data(), buffer.size());
        t.assert_equal("I/O mode after read", use_direct_io, file.has_direct_io());
        t.assert_true("read expected bytes", matches(buffer.data(), range.first, buffer.size()));
        t.assert_equal(range.first + range.second, file.tell());
    }

    std::vector<uint8_t> buffer(test_io_size - 1);
    file.seek(0, SEEK_SET);
    for (size_t offset = 0; offset < test_file_size; offset += buffer.size()) {
        const size_t size = std::min(buffer.size(), test_file_size - offset);
        file.read_raw(buffer.data(), size);
        t.assert_equal("I/O mode after sequential read", use_direct_io, file.has_direct_io());
        t.assert_true("sequential read expected bytes", matches(buffer.data(), offset, size));
        t.assert_equal(offset + size, file.tell());
    }

    const size_t unsafe_size = ((LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE + 3 * alignment - 1) / alignment) * alignment;
    aligned_buffer buffer_aligned(alignment, unsafe_size);
    t.assert_true("allocate aligned buffer", buffer_aligned.data != nullptr);
    if (buffer_aligned.data != nullptr) {
        const size_t offset = 7 * alignment;
        file.seek(offset, SEEK_SET);
        file.read_raw_unsafe(buffer_aligned.data, unsafe_size);
        t.assert_equal("I/O mode after unsafe read", use_direct_io, file.has_direct_io());
        t.assert_true("unsafe read expected bytes", matches(buffer_aligned.data, offset, unsafe_size));
        t.assert_equal(offset + unsafe_size, file.tell());

        const size_t final_offset = (test_file_size - 1) & ~(alignment - 1);
        file.seek(final_offset, SEEK_SET);
        file.read_raw_unsafe(buffer_aligned.data, alignment);
        t.assert_equal("I/O mode after unsafe EOF read", use_direct_io, file.has_direct_io());
        t.assert_true("unsafe EOF read expected bytes", matches(buffer_aligned.data, final_offset, test_file_size - final_offset));
        t.assert_equal(test_file_size, file.tell());
    }
}

static void check_direct_io_fallback(testing & t, const std::filesystem::path & path) {
    llama_file file(path.string().c_str(), "rb", true);
    if (!file.has_direct_io()) {
        t.skip("direct I/O is unavailable for the test file");
        return;
    }
    t.assert_true("direct I/O is enabled before fallback", file.has_direct_io());

    const size_t alignment = file.read_alignment();
    aligned_buffer buffer(alignment, alignment + 1);
    if (!t.assert_true("allocate aligned buffer", buffer.data != nullptr)) {
        return;
    }
    auto * unaligned = static_cast<uint8_t *>(buffer.data) + 1;
    const size_t offset = 7 * alignment;
    file.seek(offset, SEEK_SET);
    file.read_raw_unsafe(unaligned, alignment);

#ifdef _WIN32
    t.assert_true("unaligned read disables direct I/O", !file.has_direct_io());
    t.assert_equal(size_t(1), file.read_alignment());
#endif
    t.assert_true("fallback read expected bytes", matches(unaligned, offset, alignment));
    t.assert_equal(offset + alignment, file.tell());

    file.read_raw(unaligned, alignment);
#ifdef _WIN32
    t.assert_true("subsequent read stays buffered", !file.has_direct_io());
#endif
    t.assert_true("subsequent read expected bytes", matches(unaligned, offset + alignment, alignment));
    t.assert_equal(offset + 2 * alignment, file.tell());
}

static void check_mmap(testing & t, const std::filesystem::path & path) {
    if (!llama_mmap::SUPPORTED) {
        return;
    }

    llama_file file(path.string().c_str(), "rb");
    llama_mmap mapping(&file, 0, false);
    t.assert_equal(test_file_size, mapping.size());
    const auto * data = static_cast<const uint8_t *>(mapping.addr());
    t.assert_true("mapping has an address", data != nullptr);
    if (data != nullptr) {
        t.assert_true("first byte", matches(data, 0, 1));
        t.assert_true("chunk boundary", matches(data + LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE - 11, LLAMA_DIRECT_IO_BUFFER_SIZE - 11, 27));
        t.assert_true("last bytes", matches(data + test_file_size - 31, test_file_size - 31, 31));
    }
}

int main() {
    testing t;
    temporary_file test_file;
    t.test("test_pattern", [&] (testing & t) { check_test_pattern(t); });
    t.test("write_test_file", [&] (testing &) { write_test_file(test_file.path); });

    t.test("llama_file.buffered", [&] (testing & t) { check_file(t, test_file.path, false); });
    t.test("llama_file.direct", [&] (testing & t) { check_file(t, test_file.path, true); });
    t.test("llama_file.direct_fallback", [&] (testing & t) { check_direct_io_fallback(t, test_file.path); });
    t.test("llama_mmap", [&] (testing & t) { check_mmap(t, test_file.path); });
    return t.summary();
}


import base64
import pytest
import requests
import struct
from utils import *

server: ServerProcess

BODY = {
    "model": "tts",
    "input": "Hello there.",
    "voice": "female",
    "seed": 42,
    "n_predict": 8,
}


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def create_server():
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinylfm3omni()


def post_audio(path: str, **extra) -> requests.Response:
    return requests.post(server.make_url(path), json={**BODY, **extra}, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)


def assert_wav(res: requests.Response, data_size: int | None = None):
    assert res.status_code == 200
    assert res.headers["Content-Type"] == "audio/wav"
    assert res.content[:4] == b"RIFF" and res.content[8:12] == b"WAVE"
    assert struct.unpack("<H", res.content[34:36])[0] == 16 # bits per sample
    pos = res.content.index(b"data")
    size = struct.unpack("<I", res.content[pos + 4:pos + 8])[0]
    assert size == (len(res.content) - pos - 8 if data_size is None else data_size)
    assert len(res.content) > pos + 8


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/v1/audio/speech", "/audio/speech"])
def test_speech_default_format_is_wav(path: str):
    server.start()
    assert_wav(post_audio(path))


def test_speech_wav_same_as_tts():
    server.start()
    res = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", response_format="wav")
    assert_wav(res)
    assert res.content == post_audio("/tts").content


def test_speech_pcm_is_signed_16_bit():
    server.start()
    res = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", response_format="pcm")
    assert res.status_code == 200
    assert res.headers["Content-Type"] == "audio/pcm;rate=24000;encoding=signed-int;bits=16"

    # /tts keeps the float samples
    ref = post_audio("/tts", response_format="pcm")
    assert ref.headers["Content-Type"] == "audio/pcm;rate=24000;encoding=float;bits=32"

    samples = struct.unpack(f"<{len(res.content) // 2}h", res.content)
    floats = struct.unpack(f"<{len(ref.content) // 4}f", ref.content)
    assert len(samples) == len(floats) > 0
    assert any(v != 0 for v in samples)
    for s, f in zip(samples, floats):
        assert abs(s - max(-1.0, min(1.0, f)) * 32767) <= 1


def test_speech_stream_pcm():
    server.start()
    res = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", response_format="pcm", stream_format="audio")
    assert res.status_code == 200
    assert res.headers.get("Transfer-Encoding") == "chunked"
    assert res.content == post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", response_format="pcm").content


def test_speech_stream_wav_has_unknown_size():
    server.start()
    res = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", stream_format="audio")
    assert_wav(res, data_size=0xFFFFFFFF)


def test_speech_voice():
    server.start()
    female = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", voice="female")
    male = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", voice="male")
    assert_wav(female)
    assert_wav(male)
    assert female.content != male.content
    assert post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", voice={"id": "male"}).content == male.content


def test_speech_multipart():
    server.start()
    fields = {k: (None, str(v)) for k, v in BODY.items()}
    res = requests.post(server.make_url("/v1/audio/speech"), files=fields, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
    assert_wav(res)
    assert res.content == post_audio("/v1/audio/speech").content


@pytest.mark.parametrize("extra", [
    {"speed": 1.0},
    {"instructions": "Speak slowly."},
])
def test_speech_accepted_params(extra: dict):
    server.start()
    assert_wav(post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", **extra))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("extra,message", [
    ({"response_format": "mp3"},  "response_format"),
    ({"response_format": "opus"}, "response_format"),
    ({"stream_format": "sse"},    "stream_format"),
    ({"speed": 1.5},              "speed"),
    ({"voice": "alloy"},          "unknown voice"),
    ({"input": ""},               "input"),
])
def test_speech_invalid_request(extra: dict, message: str):
    server.start()
    res = post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", **extra)
    assert res.status_code == 400
    assert message in res.json()["error"]["message"]


def test_speech_no_tts_model():
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinyllama2()
    server.start()
    assert post_audio("/v1/audio/speech").status_code == 501 # ERROR_TYPE_NOT_SUPPORTED


def test_speech_router():
    global server
    model = server.model_hf_repo
    server = ServerPreset.router()
    server.start()
    assert_wav(post_audio("/v1/audio/speech", model=model))


#
# tts only model, with voice cloning
#

def pocket_tts(path: str = "/v1/audio/speech", **extra) -> requests.Response:
    body = {"input": "Hello there.", "seed": 42, "n_predict": 8, **extra}
    return requests.post(server.make_url(path), json=body, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)


def test_tts_only():
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinypockettts()
    server.start()
    res = server.make_request("GET", "/props")
    assert not res.body.get("voices") # this slice has no predefined voices
    res = server.make_request("GET", "/v1/models")
    assert res.body["data"][0]["architecture"]["output_modalities"] == ["audio"]
    res = pocket_tts()
    assert_wav(res)
    assert res.content == pocket_tts().content


def test_tts_only_speaker_ref():
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinypockettts()
    server.start()
    ref_a = make_wav(seconds=2.0, rate=24000, freq=220)
    ref_b = make_wav(seconds=2.0, rate=24000, freq=440)
    res = pocket_tts("/tts", speaker_ref_b64=base64.b64encode(ref_a).decode())
    assert_wav(res)
    assert res.content != pocket_tts("/tts").content
    assert res.content != pocket_tts("/tts", speaker_ref_b64=base64.b64encode(ref_b).decode()).content
    # the same reference as an uploaded file
    fields = {"input": (None, "Hello there."), "seed": (None, "42"), "n_predict": (None, "8"), "speaker_ref": ("ref.wav", ref_a)}
    res_file = requests.post(server.make_url("/tts"), files=fields, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
    assert res_file.content == res.content
    # /v1/audio/speech passes it through
    assert pocket_tts(speaker_ref_b64=base64.b64encode(ref_a).decode()).content == res.content


@pytest.mark.parametrize("speaker_ref_b64,message", [
    ("!!!", "not valid base64"),
    (base64.b64encode(b"not an audio file").decode(), "failed to decode"),
])
def test_tts_only_invalid_speaker_ref(speaker_ref_b64: str, message: str):
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinypockettts()
    server.start()
    res = pocket_tts("/tts", speaker_ref_b64=speaker_ref_b64)
    assert res.status_code == 400
    assert message in res.json()["error"]["message"]


def test_tts_only_end_of_speech():
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinypockettts()
    server.start()
    # the end-of-speech head stops the generation before n_predict
    res = pocket_tts(n_predict=16)
    assert_wav(res)
    assert res.content == pocket_tts(n_predict=1000).content


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path", ["/v1/chat/completions", "/v1/audio/transcriptions"])
def test_tts_only_no_text_output(path: str):
    global server
    server = ServerPreset.tinypockettts()
    server.start()
    if path == "/v1/audio/transcriptions":
        res = requests.post(server.make_url(path), files={"file": ("audio.wav", make_wav())}, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
    else:
        res = requests.post(server.make_url(path), json={"messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hello"}]}, timeout=DEFAULT_REQUEST_TIMEOUT)
    assert res.status_code == 501


#include "ggml-backend.h"
#include "ggml-rpc.h"
#include "ggml.h"

#include <cstdio>

// Regression test: an unreachable or malformed RPC endpoint must be reported as an
// error instead of aborting the calling process.
//
// Before the fix, ggml_backend_rpc_add_server() called GGML_ABORT (SIGABRT) when the
// endpoint could not be parsed, the transport failed to initialize, the socket could
// not connect, or the HELLO handshake failed - so a typo in an endpoint or a node
// that was still booting killed the whole application, and this test would die
// instead of failing cleanly.
//
// After the fix it returns nullptr.
//
// ref: https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/30415

int main(void) {
    ggml_backend_load_all();

    // 1) nothing listens on this port: the connect must fail, not abort
    ggml_backend_reg_t reg = ggml_backend_rpc_add_server("127.0.0.1:1");
    GGML_ASSERT(reg == nullptr);

    // 2) a malformed endpoint must take the parse-failure path and also return nullptr
    reg = ggml_backend_rpc_add_server("this-is-not-an-endpoint");
    GGML_ASSERT(reg == nullptr);

    printf("ok\n");
    return 0;
}


#version 450

#include "types.glsl"
#include "generic_binary_head.glsl"

#define TILE_DIM 32
layout(local_size_x = TILE_DIM, local_size_y = 8, local_size_z = 1) in;

// 33 columns (not 32) pads away shared-memory bank conflicts.
shared A_TYPE tile[TILE_DIM][TILE_DIM + 1];

void main() {
    const uint tid_x = gl_LocalInvocationID.x;
    const uint tid_y = gl_LocalInvocationID.y;

    // workgroup x walks rows (src1's contiguous dim1) so consecutive workgroups stream src1, y walks dst columns
    const uint row0 = gl_WorkGroupID.x * TILE_DIM;
    const uint col0 = (gl_WorkGroupID.z * gl_NumWorkGroups.y + gl_WorkGroupID.y) * TILE_DIM;

    const uint i1 = row0 + tid_x;
    [[unroll]] for (uint j = 0; j < TILE_DIM; j += gl_WorkGroupSize.y) {
        const uint i0 = col0 + tid_y + j;
        if (i0 >= p.ne00 && i0 - p.ne00 < p.ne10 && i1 < p.ne11) {
            tile[tid_y + j][tid_x] = A_TYPE(data_b[get_boffset() + (i0 - p.ne00)*p.nb10 + i1*p.nb11]);
        }
    }

    barrier();

    const uint d0 = col0 + tid_x;
    [[unroll]] for (uint j = 0; j < TILE_DIM; j += gl_WorkGroupSize.y) {
        const uint d1 = row0 + tid_y + j;
        if (d0 < p.ne20 && d1 < p.ne21) {
            data_d[get_doffset() + d1*p.nb21 + d0*p.nb20] = d0 < p.ne00
                ? D_TYPE(data_a[get_aoffset() + d1*p.nb01 + d0*p.nb00])
                : D_TYPE(tile[tid_x][tid_y + j]);
        }
    }
}


#include "llama-kv-cache.h"
#include "llama-model.h"

#include <numeric>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

static void init_model(llama_model & model, uint32_t n_layer) {
    model.hparams = {};
    model.hparams.n_layer_all = n_layer;
    model.hparams.n_embd_head_k_full = 4;
    model.hparams.n_embd_head_v_full = 4;
    model.hparams.n_head_arr.fill(1);
    model.hparams.n_head_kv_arr.fill(1);
    std::iota(model.hparams.kv_source_impl.begin(), model.hparams.kv_source_impl.end(), 0);
    model.layers.resize(n_layer);
}

int main() {
    for (int bit = 0; bit < 64; ++bit) {
        GGML_ASSERT(ctz64(uint64_t{1} << bit) == bit);
        GGML_ASSERT(ctz64(UINT64_MAX << bit) == bit);
    }

    llama_backend_init();

    {
        std::string errors;
        llama_log_set([](ggml_log_level level, const char * text, void * user_data) {
            if (level == GGML_LOG_LEVEL_ERROR) {
                *static_cast<std::string *>(user_data) += text;
            }
        }, &errors);

        for (llm_arch arch : { LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK2, LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK32, LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK4,
                LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK41, LLM_ARCH_GLM_DSA, LLM_ARCH_GLM5_NEXT, LLM_ARCH_DOTS3NOTE,
                LLM_ARCH_MISTRAL_LARGE4, LLM_ARCH_HY_V4, LLM_ARCH_MINIMAX_M3, LLM_ARCH_QWEN4EXP,
                LLM_ARCH_DFLASH }) {
            std::unique_ptr<llama_model> model(llama_model_create(arch, llama_model_default_params()));
            init_model(*model, 1);
            if (arch == LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK2) {
                model->hparams.n_embd_head_k_mla_impl = 4;
                model->hparams.n_embd_head_v_mla_impl = 4;
            }

            for (ggml_type type_k : { GGML_TYPE_F16, GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 }) {
                for (ggml_type type_v : { GGML_TYPE_F16, GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 }) {
                    if (type_k == GGML_TYPE_F16 && type_v == GGML_TYPE_F16) {
                        continue;
                    }
                    auto params = llama_context_default_params();
                    params.type_k = type_k;
                    params.type_v = type_v;
                    errors.clear();
                    llama_context * ctx = llama_init_from_model(model.get(), params);
                    GGML_ASSERT(ctx == nullptr);
                    if (type_k == type_v || (!model->hparams.is_mla() && arch != LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK4 && arch != LLM_ARCH_DEEPSEEK41)) {
                        GGML_ASSERT(errors.find("FP8 cache is not supported for architecture") != std::string::npos);
                    } else {
                        GGML_ASSERT(errors.find("model does not support different K") != std::string::npos);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        llama_log_set(nullptr, nullptr);
    }

    {
        std::unique_ptr<llama_model> target(llama_model_create(LLM_ARCH_GEMMA4, llama_model_default_params()));
        std::unique_ptr<llama_model> assistant(llama_model_create(LLM_ARCH_GEMMA4_ASSISTANT, llama_model_default_params()));
        init_model(*target, 3);
        init_model(*assistant, 2);

        ggml_context_ptr ctx(ggml_init({ 4096, nullptr, false }));
        target->layers[2].k_cache_s = ggml_new_tensor_1d(ctx.get(), GGML_TYPE_F32, 1);
        target->layers[2].v_cache_s = ggml_new_tensor_1d(ctx.get(), GGML_TYPE_F32, 1);
        assistant->layers[0].k_cache_s = ggml_new_tensor_1d(ctx.get(), GGML_TYPE_F32, 1);
        assistant->layers[0].v_cache_s = ggml_new_tensor_1d(ctx.get(), GGML_TYPE_F32, 1);
        assistant->layers[1].k_cache_s = assistant->layers[0].k_cache_s;
        assistant->layers[1].v_cache_s = assistant->layers[0].v_cache_s;
        *static_cast<float *>(target->layers[2].k_cache_s->data) = 0.5f;
        *static_cast<float *>(target->layers[2].v_cache_s->data) = 0.25f;
        *static_cast<float *>(assistant->layers[0].k_cache_s->data) = 2.0f;
        *static_cast<float *>(assistant->layers[0].v_cache_s->data) = 4.0f;

        for (ggml_type type_k : { GGML_TYPE_F16, GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 }) {
            for (ggml_type type_v : { GGML_TYPE_F16, GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 }) {
                llama_kv_cache source(*target, false, target->hparams, type_k, type_v,
                        false, false, true, 32, 1, 0, LLAMA_SWA_TYPE_NONE, nullptr, nullptr,
                        [](uint32_t il) { return il == 0 ? 2 : -1; }, nullptr);
                llama_kv_cache shared(*assistant, false, assistant->hparams, type_k, type_v,
                        false, false, true, 32, 1, 0, LLAMA_SWA_TYPE_NONE, &source, nullptr, nullptr,
                        [](int32_t il) { return il == 0 ? 2 : 1; });

                GGML_ASSERT(source.get_k_scale(2) == (type_k == GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 ? target->layers[2].k_cache_s : nullptr));
                GGML_ASSERT(source.get_v_scale(2) == (type_v == GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 ? target->layers[2].v_cache_s : nullptr));
                GGML_ASSERT(source.get_k_scale(0) == source.get_k_scale(2));
                GGML_ASSERT(source.get_v_scale(0) == source.get_v_scale(2));
                GGML_ASSERT(shared.get_k_storage(0) == source.get_k_storage(2));
                GGML_ASSERT(shared.get_k_scale(0) == source.get_k_scale(2));
                GGML_ASSERT(shared.get_v_scale(0) == source.get_v_scale(2));
                GGML_ASSERT(shared.get_k_scale(1) == nullptr);
                GGML_ASSERT(shared.get_v_scale(1) == nullptr);
            }
        }
    }

    // seq_rm removes [p0, p1) of one sequence, a negative bound is open
    {
        std::unique_ptr<llama_model> model(llama_model_create(LLM_ARCH_LLAMA, llama_model_default_params()));
        init_model(*model, 1);

        llama_kv_cache kv(*model, false, model->hparams, GGML_TYPE_F16, GGML_TYPE_F16,
                false, false, true, 32, 2, 0, LLAMA_SWA_TYPE_NONE, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr);

        // returns the first cell that takes the tokens
        auto add = [&](llama_seq_id seq_id, llama_pos p0, uint32_t n) {
            std::vector<llama_token>    token(n, 1);
            std::vector<llama_pos>      pos(n);
            std::vector<int32_t>        n_seq_id(n, 1);
            std::vector<llama_seq_id *> seq_ids(n, &seq_id);
            std::iota(pos.begin(), pos.end(), p0);

            llama_ubatch ubatch = {};
            ubatch.n_tokens     = n;
            ubatch.n_seq_tokens = n;
            ubatch.n_seqs       = 1;
            ubatch.n_seqs_unq   = 1;
            ubatch.n_pos        = 1;
            ubatch.token        = token.data();
            ubatch.pos          = pos.data();
            ubatch.n_seq_id     = n_seq_id.data();
            ubatch.seq_id       = seq_ids.data();
            ubatch.seq_id_unq   = &seq_id;

            const auto sinfo = kv.find_slot(ubatch, false);
            GGML_ASSERT(!sinfo.empty());
            kv.apply_ubatch(sinfo, ubatch);

            return sinfo.head();
        };

        GGML_ASSERT(add(0, 0, 8) == 0);
        GGML_ASSERT(add(1, 0, 4) == 8);

        // the tail of sequence 0, the other sequence keeps its cells
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, 5, -1));
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_min(0) == 0 && kv.seq_pos_max(0) == 4);
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_min(1) == 0 && kv.seq_pos_max(1) == 3);

        // the next tokens go to the lowest freed cell
        GGML_ASSERT(add(0, 5, 2) == 5);

        // p1 is not part of the range
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, -1, 2));
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_min(0) == 2 && kv.seq_pos_max(0) == 6);
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, 6, 6));
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_max(0) == 6);

        // freed cells are taken in cell order, so a higher position can sit in a lower cell
        GGML_ASSERT(add(0, 7, 3) == 0);
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, 6, -1));
        GGML_ASSERT(add(0, 6, 1) == 0);

        // a cell shared with another sequence stays for that sequence
        kv.seq_cp(0, 1, 5, -1);
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, 5, -1));
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_max(0) == 4 && kv.seq_pos_max(1) == 6);

        // every cell of a repeated position is removed
        add(0, 5, 1);
        add(0, 5, 1);
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_rm(0, 5, 6));
        GGML_ASSERT(kv.seq_pos_max(0) == 4);
    }

    llama_backend_free();
    return 0;
}


"""Test KV cache scales with synthetic tensors; no GPU or model downloads required.

Install dependencies and run from the repository root:
    python -m pip install -r requirements/requirements-convert_hf_to_gguf.txt
    python -m pip install -e ./gguf-py
    python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_kv_cache_scales.py' -v

Use unittest discovery so the repository root is available for imports.
"""

import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

import torch

from conversion.base import ModelBase
import gguf


class TestKVCacheScales(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_projection_names(self):
        cases = (
            (gguf.MODEL_ARCH.PHI3, "model.layers.2.self_attn.qkv_proj", "model.layers.2.self_attn.qkv_proj"),
            (gguf.MODEL_ARCH.GPT2, "transformer.h.2.attn.c_attn", "transformer.h.2.attn.c_attn"),
            (gguf.MODEL_ARCH.LLAMA, "model.layers.2.self_attn.k_proj", "model.layers.2.self_attn.v_proj"),
        )
        for arch, k_name, v_name in cases:
            for dtype in (torch.float32, torch.bfloat16):
                with self.subTest(arch=arch, dtype=dtype), tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
                    path = Path(tmp) / "scales.gguf"
                    model = object.__new__(ModelBase)
                    model.tensor_map = gguf.get_tensor_name_map(arch, 3)
                    model.gguf_writer = gguf.GGUFWriter(path, gguf.MODEL_ARCH_NAMES[arch])
                    model.model_tensors = {
                        k_name + ".k_scale": lambda: torch.tensor(0.5, dtype=dtype),
                        v_name + ".v_scale": lambda: torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=dtype),
                    }
                    model._prepare_kv_cache_scales()
                    self.assertFalse(model.model_tensors)
                    model.gguf_writer.write_header_to_file()
                    model.gguf_writer.write_kv_data_to_file()
                    model.gguf_writer.write_tensors_to_file()
                    model.gguf_writer.close()

                    # Release the reader's memory map before Windows removes the temporary directory.
                    self._check_scales(path)

    def _check_scales(self, path):
        reader = gguf.GGUFReader(path)
        self.assertEqual([t.name for t in reader.tensors], ["blk.2.attn_k.k_scale", "blk.2.attn_v.v_scale"])
        self.assertEqual([t.tensor_type for t in reader.tensors], [gguf.GGMLQuantizationType.F32] * 2)
        self.assertEqual([float(t.data[0]) for t in reader.tensors], [0.5, 1.0])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


#include "fp8.cuh"

#if !defined(GGML_USE_HIP) && !defined(GGML_USE_MUSA) && CUDART_VERSION >= 11080

struct fp8_abs_src {
    const float * x;
    int64_t ne0;
    int64_t ne1;
    int64_t ne2;
    int64_t s1;
    int64_t s2;
    int64_t s3;

    __device__ float operator()(int64_t i) const {
        const int64_t i0 = i % ne0;
        i /= ne0;
        const int64_t i1 = i % ne1;
        i /= ne1;
        const int64_t i2 = i % ne2;
        const int64_t i3 = i / ne2;
        const float value = fabsf(x[i0 + i1*s1 + i2*s2 + i3*s3]);
        return isfinite(value) ? value : 448.0f;
    }
};

static __global__ void fp8_amax_partials(fp8_abs_src src, int64_t ne, float * partials) {
    float amax = 0.0f;
    for (int64_t i = (int64_t) blockIdx.x*blockDim.x + threadIdx.x; i < ne; i += (int64_t) blockDim.x*gridDim.x) {
        amax = fmaxf(amax, src(i));
    }

    __shared__ float shared[WARP_SIZE];
    amax = block_reduce<block_reduce_method::MAX, 256>(amax, shared);
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        partials[blockIdx.x] = amax;
    }
}

static __global__ void fp8_amax_final(const float * partials, int n, float * amax) {
    float value = 0.0f;
    for (int i = threadIdx.x; i < n; i += blockDim.x) {
        value = fmaxf(value, partials[i]);
    }

    __shared__ float shared[WARP_SIZE];
    value = block_reduce<block_reduce_method::MAX, 256>(value, shared);
    if (threadIdx.x == 0) {
        *amax = value;
    }
}

static __global__ void quantize_fp8_e4m3(
        const float * __restrict__ x, uint8_t * __restrict__ y, const float * __restrict__ amax,
        float * __restrict__ scale, int64_t ne0, int64_t ne1, int64_t ne2, int64_t ne, int64_t s1, int64_t s2, int64_t s3) {
    const int64_t i = (int64_t) blockIdx.x*blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
    if (i >= ne) {
        return;
    }

    int64_t tmp = i / ne0;
    const int64_t i0 = i - tmp*ne0;
    const int64_t i1 = tmp % ne1;
    tmp /= ne1;
    const int64_t i2 = tmp % ne2;
    const int64_t i3 = tmp / ne2;

    const float d = *amax > 0.0f ? *amax / 448.0f : 1.0f;
    y[i] = ggml_cuda_fp32_to_f8_e4m3(x[i0 + i1*s1 + i2*s2 + i3*s3] / d);
    if (i == 0) {
        *scale = d;
    }
}

static void fp8_set_batch(cublasLtMatrixLayout_t desc, int32_t count, int64_t stride) {
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutSetAttribute(desc, CUBLASLT_MATRIX_LAYOUT_BATCH_COUNT, &count, sizeof(count)));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutSetAttribute(desc, CUBLASLT_MATRIX_LAYOUT_STRIDED_BATCH_OFFSET, &stride, sizeof(stride)));
}

static void fp8_destroy_matmul(
        cublasLtMatmulDesc_t op_desc, cublasLtMatrixLayout_t a_desc, cublasLtMatrixLayout_t b_desc,
        cublasLtMatrixLayout_t d_desc) {
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutDestroy(d_desc));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutDestroy(b_desc));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutDestroy(a_desc));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulDescDestroy(op_desc));
}

bool ggml_cuda_mul_mat_fp8(
        ggml_backend_cuda_context & ctx, const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, ggml_tensor * dst,
        const ggml_tensor * weight_scale) {
    const int cc = ggml_cuda_info().devices[ctx.device].cc;
    if (!fp8_mma_hardware_available(cc) || src0->type != GGML_TYPE_F8_E4M3 || src1->type != GGML_TYPE_F32 ||
            dst->type != GGML_TYPE_F32 || !ggml_is_contiguous(dst) || src0->ne[0] % 16 != 0 || src0->ne[1] % 16 != 0 ||
            src0->nb[0] != sizeof(uint8_t) || src0->nb[1] != (size_t) src0->ne[0] || src1->nb[0] != sizeof(float) ||
            !ggml_cuda_is_aligned(src0, 16) || !ggml_cuda_is_aligned(dst, 16)) {
        return false;
    }

    GGML_ASSERT(!weight_scale || (weight_scale->type == GGML_TYPE_F32 && ggml_is_contiguous(weight_scale) && ggml_nelements(weight_scale) == 1));

    GGML_TENSOR_BINARY_OP_LOCALS
    GGML_ASSERT(ne10 == ne00);
    GGML_ASSERT(ne0 == ne01);
    GGML_ASSERT(ne12 % ne02 == 0);
    GGML_ASSERT(ne13 % ne03 == 0);

    cudaStream_t stream = ctx.stream();
    const int64_t ne_src1 = ggml_nelements(src1);
    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<uint8_t> src1_fp8(ctx.pool(), ne_src1);
    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<float> src1_scale(ctx.pool(), 1);
    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<float> src1_amax(ctx.pool(), 1);

    const fp8_abs_src abs_src = {
        (const float *) src1->data, ne10, ne11, ne12,
        (int64_t) (nb11 / sizeof(float)), (int64_t) (nb12 / sizeof(float)), (int64_t) (nb13 / sizeof(float))
    };
    const int reduce_blocks = std::min<int64_t>((ne_src1 + 255)/256, 1024);
    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<float> reduce_tmp(ctx.pool(), reduce_blocks);
    fp8_amax_partials<<<reduce_blocks, 256, 0, stream>>>(abs_src, ne_src1, reduce_tmp.ptr);
    fp8_amax_final<<<1, 256, 0, stream>>>(reduce_tmp.ptr, reduce_blocks, src1_amax.ptr);

    quantize_fp8_e4m3<<<(ne_src1 + 255)/256, 256, 0, stream>>>(
        (const float *) src1->data, src1_fp8.ptr, src1_amax.ptr, src1_scale.ptr, ne10, ne11, ne12, ne_src1,
        nb11 / sizeof(float), nb12 / sizeof(float), nb13 / sizeof(float));
    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());

    cublasLtMatmulDesc_t op_desc;
    cublasLtMatrixLayout_t a_desc;
    cublasLtMatrixLayout_t b_desc;
    cublasLtMatrixLayout_t d_desc;
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulDescCreate(&op_desc, CUBLAS_COMPUTE_32F, CUDA_R_32F));
    const cublasOperation_t trans_a = CUBLAS_OP_T;
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulDescSetAttribute(op_desc, CUBLASLT_MATMUL_DESC_TRANSA, &trans_a, sizeof(trans_a)));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulDescSetAttribute(
        op_desc, CUBLASLT_MATMUL_DESC_B_SCALE_POINTER, &src1_scale.ptr, sizeof(src1_scale.ptr)));
    if (weight_scale) {
        const float * scale_ptr = (const float *) weight_scale->data;
        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulDescSetAttribute(
            op_desc, CUBLASLT_MATMUL_DESC_A_SCALE_POINTER, &scale_ptr, sizeof(scale_ptr)));
    }
    const int64_t r2 = ne12 / ne02;
    const int64_t r3 = ne13 / ne03;

    // src1 batches that share a src0 matrix are adjacent in the compact src1 copy and in dst, fold them into N
    const int64_t n = ne11*(ne02 == 1 ? r2*r3 : r2);

    // batch the remaining matmuls with a fixed src0 stride, or issue one batched call per i3 if there is none
    int64_t batch_count = ne02;
    int64_t stride_a    = nb02;
    int64_t n_calls     = ne13;
    if (ne02 == 1) {
        batch_count = ne03;
        stride_a    = nb03;
        n_calls     = 1;
    } else if (r3 == 1 && (ne03 == 1 || nb03 == ne02*nb02)) {
        batch_count = ne02*ne03;
        n_calls     = 1;
    }

    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutCreate(&a_desc, CUDA_R_8F_E4M3, ne00, ne01, ne00));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutCreate(&b_desc, CUDA_R_8F_E4M3, ne10, n, ne10));
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatrixLayoutCreate(&d_desc, CUDA_R_32F, ne0, n, ne0));
    if (batch_count > 1) {
        fp8_set_batch(a_desc, batch_count, stride_a);
        fp8_set_batch(b_desc, batch_count, ne10*n);
        fp8_set_batch(d_desc, batch_count, ne0*n);
    }

    // TODO: Allow workspace in the heuristic search for potential performance gains.
    // cuBLASLt takes caller-owned workspace per matmul call.
    cublasLtMatmulPreference_t preference;
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulPreferenceCreate(&preference));
    cublasLtMatmulHeuristicResult_t heuristic;
    int returned = 0;
    const cublasStatus_t status = cublasLtMatmulAlgoGetHeuristic(
        ctx.cublaslt_handle(), op_desc, a_desc, b_desc, d_desc, d_desc, preference, 1, &heuristic, &returned);
    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmulPreferenceDestroy(preference));
    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS || returned == 0) {
        fp8_destroy_matmul(op_desc, a_desc, b_desc, d_desc);
        return false;
    }

    const float alpha = 1.0f;
    const float beta = 0.0f;
    for (int64_t i3 = 0; i3 < n_calls; ++i3) {
        const char * a = (const char *) src0->data + (i3/r3)*nb03;
        const uint8_t * b = src1_fp8.ptr + i3*ne12*ne11*ne10;
        float * d = (float *) ((char *) dst->data + i3*dst->nb[3]);
        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasLtMatmul(ctx.cublaslt_handle(), op_desc, &alpha, a, a_desc, b, b_desc,
            &beta, d, d_desc, d, d_desc, &heuristic.algo, nullptr, 0, stream));
    }

    fp8_destroy_matmul(op_desc, a_desc, b_desc, d_desc);
    return true;
}

#else

bool ggml_cuda_mul_mat_fp8(
        ggml_backend_cuda_context & ctx, const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, ggml_tensor * dst,
        const ggml_tensor * weight_scale) {
    GGML_UNUSED_VARS(ctx, src0, src1, dst, weight_scale);
    return false;
}

#endif


#include "chat-tts-norm.h"

#include "jinja/runtime.h"
#include "jinja/value.h"
#include "json.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <climits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

// TTS normalization patterns, see docs/tts-norm-patterns.md for the specification
// keep this file in sync with the python reference: scripts/chat_tts_norm.py

using namespace jinja;

//
// character classes
// they are defined with explicit codepoint ranges, independent of any unicode database version,
// so that the C++ and python implementations agree on every codepoint
//

struct tts_norm_range {
    uint32_t lo;
    uint32_t hi;
};

static const tts_norm_range TTS_NORM_SPACE[] = {
    {0x0009, 0x000D}, {0x0020, 0x0020}, {0x0085, 0x0085}, {0x00A0, 0x00A0}, {0x1680, 0x1680},
    {0x2000, 0x200A}, {0x2028, 0x2029}, {0x202F, 0x202F}, {0x205F, 0x205F}, {0x3000, 0x3000},
};

static const tts_norm_range TTS_NORM_PUNCT[] = {
    {0x0021, 0x002F}, {0x003A, 0x0040}, {0x005B, 0x0060}, {0x007B, 0x007E},
    {0x00A1, 0x00A9}, {0x00AB, 0x00B4}, {0x00B6, 0x00B9}, {0x00BB, 0x00BF}, {0x00D7, 0x00D7}, {0x00F7, 0x00F7},
    {0x2010, 0x2027}, {0x2030, 0x205E}, {0x20A0, 0x20CF}, {0x2100, 0x218F}, {0x2190, 0x2BFF},
    {0x3001, 0x3003}, {0x3008, 0x3020}, {0x3030, 0x3030}, {0x303D, 0x303D}, {0x30FB, 0x30FB},
    {0xFE10, 0xFE19}, {0xFE30, 0xFE4F}, {0xFE50, 0xFE6B},
    {0xFF01, 0xFF0F}, {0xFF1A, 0xFF20}, {0xFF3B, 0xFF40}, {0xFF5B, 0xFF65},
    {0x1F000, 0x1FAFF},
};

static const tts_norm_range TTS_NORM_CONTROL[] = {
    {0x0000, 0x001F}, {0x007F, 0x009F}, {0x200B, 0x200F}, {0x2060, 0x206F}, {0xFEFF, 0xFEFF},
};

static const tts_norm_range TTS_NORM_HAN[] = {
    {0x2E80, 0x2FDF}, {0x3005, 0x3005}, {0x3007, 0x3007}, {0x3021, 0x3029}, {0x3038, 0x303B},
    {0x3400, 0x4DBF}, {0x4E00, 0x9FFF}, {0xF900, 0xFAFF}, {0x20000, 0x323AF},
};

template<size_t N>
static bool tts_norm_in(const tts_norm_range (&ranges)[N], uint32_t c) {
    for (const auto & r : ranges) {
        if (c >= r.lo && c <= r.hi) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

static bool tts_norm_is_alpha(uint32_t c) {
    if ((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z')) {
        return true;
    }
    if (c == 0xAA || c == 0xB5 || c == 0xBA) {
        return true;
    }
    return c >= 0xC0 && !tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_SPACE, c) && !tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_PUNCT, c) && !tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_CONTROL, c);
}

// kind is one of "dspawulhc"
static bool tts_norm_class_match(char kind, uint32_t c) {
    switch (kind) {
        case 'd': return c >= '0' && c <= '9';
        case 's': return tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_SPACE, c);
        case 'p': return tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_PUNCT, c);
        case 'a': return tts_norm_is_alpha(c);
        case 'w': return tts_norm_is_alpha(c) || (c >= '0' && c <= '9');
        case 'u': return c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z';
        case 'l': return c >= 'a' && c <= 'z';
        case 'h': return tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_HAN, c);
        case 'c': return tts_norm_in(TTS_NORM_CONTROL, c);
    }
    return false;
}

static bool tts_norm_is_class_letter(uint32_t c) {
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') {
        c += 'a' - 'A';
    }
    return c < 0x80 && std::string("dspawulhc").find(static_cast<char>(c)) != std::string::npos;
}

static uint32_t tts_norm_ascii_lower(uint32_t c) {
    return (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ? c + ('a' - 'A') : c;
}

static uint32_t tts_norm_ascii_swapcase(uint32_t c) {
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') return c + ('a' - 'A');
    if (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') return c - ('a' - 'A');
    return c;
}

static bool tts_norm_is_ascii_alnum(uint32_t c) {
    return (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z');
}

// invalid bytes are decoded as U+FFFD, one per byte; offs[i] is the byte offset of codepoint i, offs[n] = text size
static void tts_norm_decode_utf8(const std::string & s, std::vector<uint32_t> & cpts, std::vector<size_t> & offs) {
    cpts.clear();
    offs.clear();
    size_t i = 0;
    const size_t n = s.size();
    while (i < n) {
        offs.push_back(i);
        const unsigned char c = static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i]);
        uint32_t cp = 0;
        size_t len = 0;
        if (c < 0x80) {
            cp = c; len = 1;
        } else if ((c & 0xE0) == 0xC0) {
            cp = c & 0x1F; len = 2;
        } else if ((c & 0xF0) == 0xE0) {
            cp = c & 0x0F; len = 3;
        } else if ((c & 0xF8) == 0xF0) {
            cp = c & 0x07; len = 4;
        }
        bool ok = len > 0 && i + len <= n;
        for (size_t k = 1; ok && k < len; k++) {
            const unsigned char cc = static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i + k]);
            if ((cc & 0xC0) != 0x80) {
                ok = false;
            } else {
                cp = (cp << 6) | (cc & 0x3F);
            }
        }
        if (!ok) {
            cpts.push_back(0xFFFD);
            i += 1;
        } else {
            cpts.push_back(cp);
            i += len;
        }
    }
    offs.push_back(n);
}

//
// AST
//

enum class tts_norm_op {
    LITERAL,    // one codepoint
    SET,        // one codepoint from a set; class escapes like %d are sets with a single class
    ANY,        // any codepoint
    START,      // ^
    END,        // $
    BOUNDARY,   // %b
    GROUP,      // (...), (?:...), (?<name>...)
    LOOKAHEAD,  // (?=...), (?!...)
    LOOKBEHIND, // (?<=c), (?<!c) - exactly one codepoint
};

struct tts_norm_class {
    char kind;
    bool neg;
};

struct tts_norm_set {
    bool neg = false;
    std::vector<std::pair<uint32_t, uint32_t>> ranges;
    std::vector<tts_norm_class> classes;
};

struct tts_norm_item;
using tts_norm_seq = std::vector<tts_norm_item>;

struct tts_norm_pattern_node {
    tts_norm_op op = tts_norm_op::GROUP;
    uint32_t cp = 0;           // LITERAL
    tts_norm_set set;               // SET
    std::vector<tts_norm_seq> alts; // GROUP, LOOKAHEAD; LOOKBEHIND holds a single item
    int capture = -1;          // GROUP: capture index, -1 for non-capturing
    bool neg = false;          // LOOKAHEAD, LOOKBEHIND
};

static constexpr int TTS_NORM_INF = INT_MAX;
static constexpr int TTS_NORM_MAX_REPEAT = 1000;
static constexpr size_t TTS_NORM_MAX_PATTERN_LEN = 4096;

struct tts_norm_item {
    tts_norm_pattern_node atom;
    int min = 1;
    int max = 1;
};

static bool tts_norm_is_zero_width(tts_norm_op op) {
    return op == tts_norm_op::START || op == tts_norm_op::END || op == tts_norm_op::BOUNDARY
        || op == tts_norm_op::LOOKAHEAD || op == tts_norm_op::LOOKBEHIND;
}

//
// parser
//

struct tts_norm_parser {
    std::vector<uint32_t> s;
    size_t p = 0;
    size_t & n_caps;

    tts_norm_parser(std::vector<uint32_t> src, size_t & n_caps) : s(std::move(src)), n_caps(n_caps) {}

    [[noreturn]] void fail(const std::string & msg) const {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid TTS norm pattern: " + msg + " at position " + std::to_string(p));
    }

    bool eof() const { return p >= s.size(); }
    uint32_t peek() const { return s[p]; }
    bool peek_is(uint32_t c) const { return !eof() && s[p] == c; }

    void expect(uint32_t c, const char * what) {
        if (!peek_is(c)) {
            fail(std::string("expected ") + what);
        }
        p++;
    }

    std::vector<tts_norm_seq> parse_alts() {
        std::vector<tts_norm_seq> alts;
        alts.push_back(parse_seq());
        while (peek_is('|')) {
            p++;
            alts.push_back(parse_seq());
        }
        return alts;
    }

    tts_norm_seq parse_seq() {
        tts_norm_seq seq;
        while (!eof() && peek() != '|' && peek() != ')') {
            tts_norm_item item;
            item.atom = parse_atom();
            parse_quantifier(item);
            seq.push_back(std::move(item));
        }
        return seq;
    }

    int parse_int() {
        if (eof() || peek() < '0' || peek() > '9') {
            fail("expected number in quantifier");
        }
        int v = 0;
        while (!eof() && peek() >= '0' && peek() <= '9') {
            v = v * 10 + static_cast<int>(peek() - '0');
            if (v > TTS_NORM_MAX_REPEAT) {
                fail("repetition count exceeds " + std::to_string(TTS_NORM_MAX_REPEAT));
            }
            p++;
        }
        return v;
    }

    void parse_quantifier(tts_norm_item & item) {
        if (eof()) {
            return;
        }
        const uint32_t c = peek();
        if (c != '?' && c != '*' && c != '+' && c != '{') {
            return;
        }
        if (tts_norm_is_zero_width(item.atom.op)) {
            fail("zero-width assertion cannot be repeated");
        }
        p++;
        if (c == '?') {
            item.min = 0; item.max = 1;
        } else if (c == '*') {
            item.min = 0; item.max = TTS_NORM_INF;
        } else if (c == '+') {
            item.min = 1; item.max = TTS_NORM_INF;
        } else {
            item.min = parse_int();
            item.max = item.min;
            if (peek_is(',')) {
                p++;
                item.max = peek_is('}') ? TTS_NORM_INF : parse_int();
            }
            expect('}', "'}' to close quantifier");
            if (item.max < item.min) {
                fail("quantifier max is smaller than min");
            }
        }
        if (!eof() && (peek() == '?' || peek() == '*' || peek() == '+' || peek() == '{')) {
            fail("multiple quantifiers");
        }
    }

    tts_norm_pattern_node parse_group() {
        // '(' already consumed
        tts_norm_pattern_node node;
        node.op = tts_norm_op::GROUP;
        if (peek_is('?')) {
            p++;
            if (eof()) {
                fail("unterminated group");
            }
            const uint32_t c = peek();
            if (c == ':') {
                p++;
            } else if (c == '=' || c == '!') {
                p++;
                node.op  = tts_norm_op::LOOKAHEAD;
                node.neg = c == '!';
            } else if (c == '<' && p + 1 < s.size() && (s[p + 1] == '=' || s[p + 1] == '!')) {
                node.op  = tts_norm_op::LOOKBEHIND;
                node.neg = s[p + 1] == '!';
                p += 2;
                tts_norm_item item;
                if (eof() || peek() == ')') {
                    fail("lookbehind must contain exactly one codepoint matcher");
                }
                item.atom = parse_atom();
                if (item.atom.op != tts_norm_op::LITERAL && item.atom.op != tts_norm_op::SET && item.atom.op != tts_norm_op::ANY) {
                    fail("lookbehind must contain exactly one codepoint matcher");
                }
                if (!peek_is(')')) {
                    fail("lookbehind must contain exactly one codepoint matcher");
                }
                p++;
                node.alts.push_back(tts_norm_seq{});
                node.alts[0].push_back(std::move(item));
                return node;
            } else if (c == 'i') {
                fail("(?i) is only allowed at the start of the pattern");
            } else {
                fail("unknown group type");
            }
        } else {
            node.capture = static_cast<int>(n_caps++);
        }
        node.alts = parse_alts();
        expect(')', "')' to close group");
        return node;
    }

    // after '%' inside or outside of a set
    // returns true if it is a class (stored in cls), false if it is a literal (stored in lit)
    bool parse_escape(bool in_set, tts_norm_class & cls, uint32_t & lit, bool & boundary) {
        boundary = false;
        if (eof()) {
            fail("dangling '%'");
        }
        const uint32_t e = s[p++];
        if (tts_norm_is_class_letter(e)) {
            cls.kind = static_cast<char>(tts_norm_ascii_lower(e));
            cls.neg  = e >= 'A' && e <= 'Z';
            return true;
        }
        if (e == 'b' && !in_set) {
            boundary = true;
            return false;
        }
        if (tts_norm_is_ascii_alnum(e)) {
            p--;
            fail(std::string("unknown escape '%") + static_cast<char>(e) + "'");
        }
        lit = e;
        return false;
    }

    tts_norm_pattern_node parse_set() {
        // '[' already consumed
        tts_norm_pattern_node node;
        node.op = tts_norm_op::SET;
        if (peek_is('^')) {
            node.set.neg = true;
            p++;
        }
        bool empty = true;
        while (true) {
            if (eof()) {
                fail("unterminated set");
            }
            if (peek() == ']') {
                p++;
                break;
            }
            empty = false;
            uint32_t lo = 0;
            if (peek() == '%') {
                p++;
                tts_norm_class cls{};
                bool boundary = false;
                if (parse_escape(true, cls, lo, boundary)) {
                    node.set.classes.push_back(cls);
                    continue;
                }
            } else {
                lo = s[p++];
            }
            uint32_t hi = lo;
            if (peek_is('-') && p + 1 < s.size() && s[p + 1] != ']') {
                p++;
                if (peek() == '%') {
                    p++;
                    tts_norm_class cls{};
                    bool boundary = false;
                    if (parse_escape(true, cls, hi, boundary)) {
                        fail("character class cannot be used in a range");
                    }
                } else {
                    hi = s[p++];
                }
                if (hi < lo) {
                    fail("invalid range in set");
                }
            }
            node.set.ranges.emplace_back(lo, hi);
        }
        if (empty) {
            fail("empty set");
        }
        return node;
    }

    tts_norm_pattern_node parse_atom() {
        const uint32_t c = s[p++];
        tts_norm_pattern_node node;
        switch (c) {
            case '(':
                return parse_group();
            case '[':
                return parse_set();
            case '.':
                node.op = tts_norm_op::ANY;
                return node;
            case '^':
                node.op = tts_norm_op::START;
                return node;
            case '$':
                node.op = tts_norm_op::END;
                return node;
            case '%': {
                tts_norm_class cls{};
                uint32_t lit = 0;
                bool boundary = false;
                if (parse_escape(false, cls, lit, boundary)) {
                    node.op = tts_norm_op::SET;
                    node.set.classes.push_back(cls);
                } else if (boundary) {
                    node.op = tts_norm_op::BOUNDARY;
                } else {
                    node.op = tts_norm_op::LITERAL;
                    node.cp = lit;
                }
                return node;
            }
            case '?':
            case '*':
            case '+':
            case '{':
                p--;
                fail("nothing to repeat");
            case ']':
            case '}':
                p--;
                fail(std::string("unescaped '") + static_cast<char>(c) + "', use '%" + static_cast<char>(c) + "'");
            default:
                node.op = tts_norm_op::LITERAL;
                node.cp = c;
                return node;
        }
    }
};

//
// matcher
// PEG-style: alternatives are ordered (the first one that matches wins) and quantifiers are greedy and possessive
// (they never give back what they consumed), so there is no backtracking
//

struct tts_norm_matcher {
    const std::vector<uint32_t> & t;
    bool icase;
    std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>> caps;

    bool is_w(size_t i) const {
        return i < t.size() && tts_norm_class_match('w', t[i]);
    }

    bool set_match(const tts_norm_set & st, uint32_t c) const {
        auto test = [&](uint32_t x) {
            for (const auto & r : st.ranges) {
                if (x >= r.first && x <= r.second) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
            for (const auto & k : st.classes) {
                if (tts_norm_class_match(k.kind, x) != k.neg) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
            return false;
        };
        bool m = test(c);
        if (!m && icase) {
            const uint32_t o = tts_norm_ascii_swapcase(c);
            if (o != c) {
                m = test(o);
            }
        }
        return m != st.neg;
    }

    bool match_alts(const std::vector<tts_norm_seq> & alts, size_t i, size_t & end) {
        for (const auto & seq : alts) {
            auto saved = caps;
            if (match_seq(seq, i, end)) {
                return true;
            }
            caps = std::move(saved);
        }
        return false;
    }

    bool match_seq(const tts_norm_seq & seq, size_t i, size_t & end) {
        size_t pos = i;
        for (const auto & it : seq) {
            int count = 0;
            while (count < it.max) {
                const bool is_group = it.atom.op == tts_norm_op::GROUP;
                std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>> saved;
                if (is_group) {
                    saved = caps;
                }
                size_t e = pos;
                if (!match_once(it.atom, pos, e)) {
                    if (is_group) {
                        caps = std::move(saved);
                    }
                    break;
                }
                if (e == pos) {
                    // an empty iteration would repeat forever, it satisfies any remaining minimum
                    count = std::max(count + 1, it.min);
                    break;
                }
                pos = e;
                count++;
            }
            if (count < it.min) {
                return false;
            }
        }
        end = pos;
        return true;
    }

    bool match_once(const tts_norm_pattern_node & node, size_t i, size_t & end) {
        const size_t n = t.size();
        switch (node.op) {
            case tts_norm_op::LITERAL:
                if (i < n && (t[i] == node.cp || (icase && tts_norm_ascii_lower(t[i]) == tts_norm_ascii_lower(node.cp)))) {
                    end = i + 1;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            case tts_norm_op::SET:
                if (i < n && set_match(node.set, t[i])) {
                    end = i + 1;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            case tts_norm_op::ANY:
                if (i < n) {
                    end = i + 1;
                    return true;
                }
                return false;
            case tts_norm_op::START:
                end = i;
                return i == 0;
            case tts_norm_op::END:
                end = i;
                return i == n;
            case tts_norm_op::BOUNDARY:
                end = i;
                return (i > 0 && is_w(i - 1)) != is_w(i);
            case tts_norm_op::GROUP: {
                size_t e = i;
                if (!match_alts(node.alts, i, e)) {
                    return false;
                }
                if (node.capture >= 0) {
                    caps[node.capture] = {static_cast<int64_t>(i), static_cast<int64_t>(e)};
                }
                end = e;
                return true;
            }
            case tts_norm_op::LOOKAHEAD: {
                // captures made inside a lookahead are discarded
                auto saved = caps;
                size_t e = i;
                const bool ok = match_alts(node.alts, i, e);
                caps = std::move(saved);
                end = i;
                return ok != node.neg;
            }
            case tts_norm_op::LOOKBEHIND: {
                size_t e = 0;
                const bool ok = i > 0 && match_once(node.alts[0][0].atom, i - 1, e) && e == i;
                end = i;
                return ok != node.neg;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
};

//
// common_tts_norm_pattern
//

common_tts_norm_pattern::common_tts_norm_pattern(const std::string & src) {
    if (src.size() > TTS_NORM_MAX_PATTERN_LEN) {
        throw std::runtime_error("invalid TTS norm pattern: pattern is longer than " + std::to_string(TTS_NORM_MAX_PATTERN_LEN) + " bytes");
    }
    std::vector<uint32_t> cpts;
    std::vector<size_t> offs;
    tts_norm_decode_utf8(src, cpts, offs);

    tts_norm_parser parser(std::move(cpts), n_caps);
    static const std::vector<uint32_t> icase_prefix = {'(', '?', 'i', ')'};
    if (parser.s.size() >= 4 && std::equal(icase_prefix.begin(), icase_prefix.end(), parser.s.begin())) {
        icase = true;
        parser.p = 4;
    }

    root = std::make_unique<tts_norm_pattern_node>();
    root->op   = tts_norm_op::GROUP;
    root->alts = parser.parse_alts();
    if (!parser.eof()) {
        parser.fail("unbalanced ')'");
    }
}

common_tts_norm_pattern::~common_tts_norm_pattern() = default;
common_tts_norm_pattern::common_tts_norm_pattern(common_tts_norm_pattern &&) noexcept = default;
common_tts_norm_pattern & common_tts_norm_pattern::operator=(common_tts_norm_pattern &&) noexcept = default;

bool common_tts_norm_pattern::match_at(const std::vector<uint32_t> & cpts, size_t pos, size_t & end, std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>> & groups) const {
    tts_norm_matcher m{cpts, icase, std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>>(n_caps, {-1, -1})};
    size_t e = pos;
    if (!m.match_alts(root->alts, pos, e)) {
        return false;
    }
    end = e;
    groups = std::move(m.caps);
    return true;
}

size_t common_tts_norm_pattern::n_groups() const {
    return n_caps;
}

//
// scan / sub
//

std::vector<common_tts_norm_segment> common_tts_norm_scan(const std::string & text, const std::vector<const common_tts_norm_pattern *> & rules) {
    std::vector<uint32_t> cpts;
    std::vector<size_t> offs;
    tts_norm_decode_utf8(text, cpts, offs);

    std::vector<common_tts_norm_segment> out;
    const size_t n = cpts.size();
    size_t i = 0;
    size_t pending = 0; // start of the current unmatched run
    std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>> groups;

    while (i < n) {
        bool matched = false;
        for (size_t r = 0; r < rules.size(); r++) {
            size_t end = i;
            if (rules[r]->match_at(cpts, i, end, groups) && end > i) {
                if (pending < i) {
                    out.push_back({-1, offs[pending], offs[i], {}});
                }
                common_tts_norm_segment seg;
                seg.rule  = static_cast<int>(r);
                seg.start = offs[i];
                seg.end   = offs[end];
                for (const auto & g : groups) {
                    if (g.first < 0) {
                        seg.groups.emplace_back(std::string::npos, std::string::npos);
                    } else {
                        seg.groups.emplace_back(offs[g.first], offs[g.second]);
                    }
                }
                out.push_back(std::move(seg));
                i = end;
                pending = i;
                matched = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!matched) {
            i++;
        }
    }
    if (pending < n) {
        out.push_back({-1, offs[pending], offs[n], {}});
    }
    return out;
}

struct tts_norm_repl_piece {
    std::string literal;
    int group = -1; // 0 = whole match, 1.. = groups
};

static std::vector<tts_norm_repl_piece> tts_norm_parse_repl(const std::string & repl, const common_tts_norm_pattern & pattern) {
    std::vector<tts_norm_repl_piece> pieces;
    std::string lit;
    auto flush = [&]() {
        if (!lit.empty()) {
            pieces.push_back({lit, -1});
            lit.clear();
        }
    };
    for (size_t i = 0; i < repl.size(); i++) {
        if (repl[i] != '%') {
            lit += repl[i];
            continue;
        }
        if (i + 1 >= repl.size()) {
            throw std::runtime_error("invalid TTS norm replacement: dangling '%'");
        }
        const char c = repl[++i];
        if (c == '%') {
            lit += '%';
        } else if (c >= '0' && c <= '9') {
            const int g = c - '0';
            if (static_cast<size_t>(g) > pattern.n_groups()) {
                throw std::runtime_error("invalid TTS norm replacement: group %" + std::string(1, c) + " does not exist");
            }
            flush();
            pieces.push_back({"", g});
        } else {
            throw std::runtime_error("invalid TTS norm replacement: unknown escape '%" + std::string(1, c) + "'");
        }
    }
    flush();
    return pieces;
}

std::string common_tts_norm_sub(const std::string & text, const common_tts_norm_pattern & pattern, const std::string & repl) {
    const auto pieces = tts_norm_parse_repl(repl, pattern);
    std::string out;
    for (const auto & seg : common_tts_norm_scan(text, {&pattern})) {
        if (seg.rule < 0) {
            out.append(text, seg.start, seg.end - seg.start);
            continue;
        }
        for (const auto & piece : pieces) {
            if (piece.group < 0) {
                out += piece.literal;
            } else if (piece.group == 0) {
                out.append(text, seg.start, seg.end - seg.start);
            } else {
                const auto & g = seg.groups[piece.group - 1];
                if (g.first != std::string::npos) {
                    out.append(text, g.first, g.second - g.first);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    return out;
}

//
// template builtins
//

// rules can be: a pattern string (rule name "match"), a dict {name: pattern}, or a list of [name, pattern] pairs
static std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> tts_norm_rules_from_value(const value & v) {
    std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> rules;
    if (is_val<value_string>(v)) {
        rules.emplace_back("match", v->as_string().str());
    } else if (is_val<value_object>(v)) {
        for (const auto & kv : v->as_ordered_object()) {
            if (!is_val<value_string>(kv.second)) {
                throw std::runtime_error("tts_norm_scan: rule pattern must be a string");
            }
            rules.emplace_back(kv.first->as_string().str(), kv.second->as_string().str());
        }
    } else if (is_val<value_array>(v)) {
        for (const auto & item : v->as_array()) {
            if (!is_val<value_array>(item) || item->as_array().size() != 2
                    || !is_val<value_string>(item->as_array()[0]) || !is_val<value_string>(item->as_array()[1])) {
                throw std::runtime_error("tts_norm_scan: each rule must be a [name, pattern] pair of strings");
            }
            rules.emplace_back(item->as_array()[0]->as_string().str(), item->as_array()[1]->as_string().str());
        }
    } else {
        throw std::runtime_error("tts_norm_scan: rules must be a string, a dict or a list of [name, pattern] pairs, got " + v->type());
    }
    return rules;
}

static value tts_norm_builtin_scan(const func_args & args) {
    args.ensure_count(2, 2);
    args.ensure_val<value_string>(args.get_pos(0));
    const string text_val = args.get_pos(0)->as_string();
    const std::string text = text_val.str();
    const bool is_input = text_val.all_parts_are_input();

    const auto rule_srcs = tts_norm_rules_from_value(args.get_pos(1));
    std::vector<common_tts_norm_pattern> patterns;
    patterns.reserve(rule_srcs.size());
    std::vector<const common_tts_norm_pattern *> ptrs;
    for (const auto & r : rule_srcs) {
        patterns.emplace_back(r.second);
    }
    for (const auto & p : patterns) {
        ptrs.push_back(&p);
    }

    auto mk_str = [&](size_t start, size_t end) -> value {
        return mk_val<value_string>(string(text.substr(start, end - start), is_input));
    };

    auto out = mk_val<value_array>();
    for (const auto & seg : common_tts_norm_scan(text, ptrs)) {
        auto groups = mk_val<value_array>();
        for (const auto & g : seg.groups) {
            // groups that did not participate are empty strings, so they render as nothing and are falsy
            groups->push_back(g.first == std::string::npos ? mk_str(0, 0) : mk_str(g.first, g.second));
        }
        auto obj = mk_val<value_object>();
        obj->insert("rule", seg.rule < 0 ? value(mk_val<value_none>()) : value(mk_val<value_string>(rule_srcs[seg.rule].first)));
        obj->insert("text", mk_str(seg.start, seg.end));
        obj->insert("groups", groups);
        out->push_back(obj);
    }
    return out;
}

static value tts_norm_builtin_sub(const func_args & args) {
    args.ensure_count(3, 3);
    args.ensure_vals<value_string, value_string, value_string>();
    const string text_val = args.get_pos(0)->as_string();
    const common_tts_norm_pattern pattern(args.get_pos(1)->as_string().str());
    const std::string res = common_tts_norm_sub(text_val.str(), pattern, args.get_pos(2)->as_string().str());
    return mk_val<value_string>(string(res, text_val.all_parts_are_input()));
}

bool common_tts_dict_find(const common_tts_dict & dict, const std::string & key, std::string & value) {
    const auto it = std::lower_bound(dict.keys.begin(), dict.keys.end(), key);
    if (it == dict.keys.end() || *it != key) {
        return false;
    }
    const size_t idx = it - dict.keys.begin();
    if (idx >= dict.values.size()) {
        throw std::runtime_error("tts_dict: malformed dictionary, a key has no value");
    }
    value = dict.values[idx];
    return true;
}

void common_chat_tts_norm_add_builtins(context & ctx, std::shared_ptr<const common_tts_dicts> dicts, common_tts_g2p g2p) {
    ctx.set_val("tts_norm_scan", mk_val<value_func>("tts_norm_scan", tts_norm_builtin_scan));
    ctx.set_val("tts_norm_sub",  mk_val<value_func>("tts_norm_sub",  tts_norm_builtin_sub));
    ctx.set_val("tts_dict",      mk_val<value_func>("tts_dict", [dicts](const func_args & args) -> value {
        args.ensure_count(2, 2);
        args.ensure_vals<value_string, value_string>();
        const std::string name = args.get_pos(0)->as_string().str();
        const auto it = dicts ? dicts->find(name) : common_tts_dicts::const_iterator();
        if (!dicts || it == dicts->end()) {
            throw std::runtime_error("tts_dict: unknown dictionary '" + name + "'");
        }
        std::string doc;
        if (!common_tts_dict_find(it->second, args.get_pos(1)->as_string().str(), doc)) {
            return mk_val<value_none>();
        }
        return value_from_json(common_json::parse(doc), false);
    }));
    // none when the caller gives no letter-to-sound model, or the model has no pronunciation
    ctx.set_val("tts_g2p",       mk_val<value_func>("tts_g2p", [g2p](const func_args & args) -> value {
        args.ensure_count(1, 1);
        args.ensure_vals<value_string>();
        const std::string out = g2p ? g2p(args.get_pos(0)->as_string().str()) : std::string();
        if (out.empty()) {
            return mk_val<value_none>();
        }
        return mk_val<value_string>(out);
    }));
}



#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <map>
#include <memory>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

// TTS normalization patterns: a small, fully specified matching language for TTS text normalization templates
//
// it is NOT a regex engine: the semantics are defined by llama.cpp (see docs/tts-norm-patterns.md), so that this
// implementation and the python reference (scripts/chat_tts_norm.py) give the same result for every input
//
// functions exposed to task templates (e.g. "tts") via common_chat_tts_norm_add_builtins():
//   tts_norm_scan(text, rules)        -> list of segments {rule, text, groups}
//   tts_norm_sub(text, pattern, repl) -> string
//   tts_dict(name, key)               -> value of key in a dictionary of the model, or none

namespace jinja {
struct context;
}

struct tts_norm_pattern_node;

class common_tts_norm_pattern {
public:
    // throws std::runtime_error on invalid pattern
    explicit common_tts_norm_pattern(const std::string & src);
    ~common_tts_norm_pattern();
    common_tts_norm_pattern(common_tts_norm_pattern &&) noexcept;
    common_tts_norm_pattern & operator=(common_tts_norm_pattern &&) noexcept;

    // match anchored at codepoint index pos; on success, end is the codepoint index after the match
    // groups[i] holds [start, end) codepoint indices of capture i+1, or {-1, -1} if the group did not participate
    bool match_at(const std::vector<uint32_t> & cpts, size_t pos, size_t & end, std::vector<std::pair<int64_t, int64_t>> & groups) const;

    size_t n_groups() const;

private:
    std::unique_ptr<tts_norm_pattern_node> root;
    size_t n_caps = 0;
    bool icase = false;
};

// segment produced by common_tts_norm_scan(); byte offsets into the input text
struct common_tts_norm_segment {
    int    rule  = -1; // index of the matched rule, -1 for unmatched text
    size_t start = 0;
    size_t end   = 0;
    std::vector<std::pair<size_t, size_t>> groups; // byte offsets; {npos, npos} for groups that did not participate
};

// split text into matched and unmatched segments
// at each position, rules are tried in order and the first non-empty match wins; otherwise one codepoint is skipped
std::vector<common_tts_norm_segment> common_tts_norm_scan(const std::string & text, const std::vector<const common_tts_norm_pattern *> & rules);

// replace every non-overlapping match of pattern with repl
// repl references: %0 (whole match), %1..%9 (groups), %% (literal percent)
std::string common_tts_norm_sub(const std::string & text, const common_tts_norm_pattern & pattern, const std::string & repl);

// a dictionary of the model, e.g. a pronunciation lexicon
struct common_tts_dict {
    std::vector<std::string> keys;   // sorted by their bytes
    std::vector<std::string> values; // the JSON document of each key
};

using common_tts_dicts = std::map<std::string, common_tts_dict>;

// binary search of key in a dictionary; on success, value is set to the JSON document
bool common_tts_dict_find(const common_tts_dict & dict, const std::string & key, std::string & value);

// pronunciation of a word by a letter-to-sound model, as phonemes separated by spaces (e.g. CMUdict notation)
// empty when there is none; the notation is the one of the model, its "tts" template knows it
using common_tts_g2p = std::function<std::string(const std::string & word)>;

// add tts_norm_scan(), tts_norm_sub(), tts_dict() and tts_g2p() to the context of a task template
// they are not jinja global builtins on purpose: chat templates are not affected
void common_chat_tts_norm_add_builtins(jinja::context & ctx, std::shared_ptr<const common_tts_dicts> dicts = nullptr,
                                       common_tts_g2p g2p = nullptr);


#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Reference implementation of the template functions tts_norm_scan(), tts_norm_sub() (TTS normalization patterns) and tts_dict().
