            const block_iq3_s * bx = (const block_iq3_s *) x[r];
            const int qh = bx->qh[s];
            // the values [4k, 4k + 4) of a sub-block come from grid entry k, with the signs in bits 4*(k%2) of byte k/2
            const int k_lo = t;
            const int k_hi = 4 + t;
            const int grid_lo = iq3s_grid[bx->qs[8*s + k_lo] | (((qh >> k_lo) & 1) << 8)];
            const int grid_hi = iq3s_grid[bx->qs[8*s + k_hi] | (((qh >> k_hi) & 1) << 8)];
            const int sign_lo = (bx->signs[4*s + k_lo/2] >> (4*(k_lo % 2))) & 0x0F;
            const int sign_hi = (bx->signs[4*s + k_hi/2] >> (4*(k_hi % 2))) & 0x0F;
            const int mask_lo = __vcmpne4(((sign_lo & 0x03) << 7) | ((sign_lo & 0x0C) << 21), 0x00000000);
            const int mask_hi = __vcmpne4(((sign_hi & 0x03) << 7) | ((sign_hi & 0x0C) << 21), 0x00000000);
            lo[r] = __vsub4(grid_lo ^ mask_lo, mask_lo);
            hi[r] = __vsub4(grid_hi ^ mask_hi, mask_hi);
            ls[r] = 1 + 2*((bx->scales[s/2] >> (4*(s % 2))) & 0x0F);
        }
        mmq_few_mma_add<GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S>(lo, hi, ls, yf, s, sum_d);
    }
    mmq_few_add_scaled(sum, d, sum_d);
}

template <ggml_type type, int ncg>
static __device__ __forceinline__ void mmq_few_vec_dot(const char * const (&x)[2], const mmq_few_y_frag (&yf)[ncg], float (&sum)[ncg][4]) {
    if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_Q8_0 || type == GGML_TYPE_IQ4_NL) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_q8_0_iq4_nl<type, ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_Q3_K) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_q3_K<ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_Q4_K || type == GGML_TYPE_Q5_K) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_q45_K<type, ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_Q6_K) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_q6_K<ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_iq3_s<ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else if constexpr (type == GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS) {
        mmq_few_vec_dot_iq4_xs<ncg>(x, yf, sum);
    } else {
        static_assert(type == GGML_TYPE_COUNT, "unsupported type");
    }
}

#endif // defined(CP_ASYNC_AVAILABLE)

// has_ids: MUL_MAT_ID, blockIdx.y is a pair of quantize_mmq_few_id_q8_1, its expert is the src0 channel and dst dims 1 and 2 are slot and token.
template <ggml_type type, int ncg, int nmt, bool has_ids>
__launch_bounds__(MMQ_FEW_NWARPS*ggml_cuda_get_physical_warp_size(), 1)
static __global__ void mul_mat_q_few(
        const char * x, const int4 * y_ptr, float * dst,
        const int nunits, const int nrows_x, const int ncols_dst, const int64_t stride_row_x, const int stride_col_dst,
        const int channel_ratio, const int64_t stride_channel_x, const int stride_channel_dst,
        const int sample_ratio, const int64_t stride_sample_x, const int stride_sample_dst, const bool x_is_constant, const int * pairs) {
#if defined(CP_ASYNC_AVAILABLE)
    constexpr int nwarps    = MMQ_FEW_NWARPS;
    constexpr int warp_size = ggml_cuda_get_physical_warp_size();
    static_assert(warp_size == WARP_SIZE, "unsupported warp size");
    static_assert(!has_ids || (ncg == 1 && nmt == 1), "unsupported MUL_MAT_ID config");

    constexpr int  unit_size    = mmq_few_unit_size(type);
    constexpr int  nrows        = nmt*MMQ_FEW_NROWS;
    constexpr int  nunits_stage = mmq_few_nunits_stage(type, ncg, nmt);
    constexpr int  nstages      = mmq_few_nstages(type);
    constexpr bool unaligned    = mmq_few_unaligned(type, nunits_stage);
    constexpr int  nchunks      = mmq_few_nchunks(type, nunits_stage);
    constexpr int  sram_stride  = mmq_few_sram_stride(type, nunits_stage);
    constexpr int  nbytes_stage = nrows*sram_stride;
    constexpr int  ncopies      = nrows*nchunks;
    static_assert(unit_size > 0, "unsupported type");

    extern __shared__ char data_mmq_few[];
    char * ring = data_mmq_few + threadIdx.y*nstages*nbytes_stage;

    const bool ksplit   = mmq_few_ksplit(has_ids, nunits);
    const int  row0_blk = blockIdx.x*(ksplit ? nrows : nwarps*nrows);
    const int  row0     = ksplit ? row0_blk : row0_blk + threadIdx.y*nrows;
    const int  iu_begin = ksplit ? nunits_stage*threadIdx.y : 0;
    const int  iu_step  = ksplit ? nunits_stage*nwarps : nunits_stage;

    const int channel_dst = blockIdx.y;
    const int sample_dst  = blockIdx.z;
    const int nchannels   = gridDim.y;

    int ncols = ncols_dst;
    [[maybe_unused]] const int * pair = nullptr;
    if constexpr (has_ids) {
        ggml_cuda_pdl_sync(); // the pairs come from the preceding kernel
        pair  = pairs + channel_dst*MMQ_FEW_PAIR_INTS;
        ncols = pair[1];
        if (ncols == 0) {
            return;
        }
        x += int64_t(pair[0])*stride_channel_x;
    } else {
        x   += int64_t(sample_dst/sample_ratio)*stride_sample_x + int64_t(channel_dst/channel_ratio)*stride_channel_x;
        dst += int64_t(sample_dst)*stride_sample_dst + int64_t(channel_dst)*stride_channel_dst;
    }
    const int4 * GGML_CUDA_RESTRICT y = y_ptr + (int64_t(sample_dst)*nchannels + channel_dst)*nunits*ncg*MMQ_FEW_Q8_1_INT4;

    // No bounds check for the units, the gate makes nunits a multiple of nunits_stage.
    // The extra bytes of unaligned data are inside the allocation, which ggml aligns and pads to 128 bytes.
    auto load_stage = [&](const int stage, const int iu0) {
        const unsigned int dst_stage = ggml_cuda_cvta_generic_to_shared(ring + stage*nbytes_stage);
#pragma unroll
        for (int c0 = 0; c0 < ncopies; c0 += warp_size) {
            const int c  = c0 + threadIdx.x;
            const int i  = c / nchunks;
            const int ic = c % nchunks;
            if (ncopies % warp_size == 0 || c < ncopies) {
                const int row = min(row0 + i, nrows_x - 1);
                const char * src = x + int64_t(row)*stride_row_x + int64_t(iu0)*unit_size;
                if constexpr (unaligned) {
                    const int off = (uintptr_t) src % 16;
                    if (16*ic < off + nunits_stage*unit_size) {
                        cp_async_cg_16<0>(dst_stage + i*sram_stride + 16*ic, src - off + 16*ic);
                    }
                } else {
                    cp_async_cg_16<0>(dst_stage + i*sram_stride + 16*ic, src + 16*ic);
                }
            }
        }
    };

    // offsets of the rows of this lane from their 16 byte boundary
    int off_row[2*nmt] = {0};
    if constexpr (unaligned) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int r = 0; r < 2*nmt; ++r) {
            const int row = min(row0 + int(threadIdx.x)/4 + 8*r, nrows_x - 1);
            off_row[r] = (uintptr_t) (x + int64_t(row)*stride_row_x) % 16;
        }
    }

    // Only weights, which no preceding kernel writes, are read before the PDL wait.
    if (!x_is_constant) {
        ggml_cuda_pdl_sync();
    }

#if __CUDA_ARCH__ == GGML_CUDA_CC_GB10
    // DGX Spark DRAM serves the short row pieces of the stage copies slowly, so the rows of the block are first prefetched into L2 in address order.
    {
        const int     tid       = threadIdx.y*warp_size + threadIdx.x;
        const int     nrows_blk = min(ksplit ? nrows : nwarps*nrows, nrows_x - row0_blk);
        const int64_t row_bytes = int64_t(nunits)*unit_size;
        const bool    contig    = stride_row_x == row_bytes;
        for (int ir = 0; ir < (contig ? 1 : nrows_blk); ++ir) {
            const uintptr_t p0 = (uintptr_t) (x + int64_t(row0_blk + ir)*stride_row_x);
            const uintptr_t p1 = p0 + (contig ? nrows_blk*row_bytes : row_bytes);
            for (uintptr_t p = (p0 & ~uintptr_t(127)) + 128*tid; p < p1; p += 128*nwarps*warp_size) {
                ggml_cuda_prefetch_l2((const void *) p);
            }
        }
    }
#endif // __CUDA_ARCH__ == GGML_CUDA_CC_GB10

#pragma unroll
    for (int stage = 0; stage < nstages - 1; ++stage) {
        const int iu0 = iu_begin + stage*iu_step;
        if (iu0 < nunits) {
            load_stage(stage, iu0);
        }
        cp_async_commit_group();
    }
    if (x_is_constant) {
        ggml_cuda_pdl_sync();
    }

    auto load_y = [&](mmq_few_y_frag (&yf)[ncg], const int iu) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int c = 0; c < ncg; ++c) {
            mmq_few_load_y(yf[c], y + (int64_t(iu)*ncg + c)*MMQ_FEW_Q8_1_INT4);
        }
    };

    // The next src1 fragments are loaded right after the MMAs that use the current ones, an earlier load is slower.
    mmq_few_y_frag yf[nunits_stage][ncg];
    if (iu_begin < nunits) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < nunits_stage; ++k) {
            load_y(yf[k], iu_begin + k);
        }
    }

    float sum[nmt][ncg][4] = {{{0.0f}}};

    for (int iter = 0, iu0 = iu_begin; iu0 < nunits; ++iter, iu0 += iu_step) {
        const int iu_load = iu0 + (nstages - 1)*iu_step;
        if (iu_load < nunits) {
            load_stage((iter + nstages - 1) % nstages, iu_load);
        }
        cp_async_commit_group();

        const bool has_next = iu0 + iu_step < nunits;

        cp_async_wait_group<nstages - 1>();
        ggml_cuda_syncwarp();

        const char * xs = ring + (iter % nstages)*nbytes_stage;
        const int off_stage = unaligned ? (iu0*unit_size) % 16 : 0;
#pragma unroll
        for (int k = 0; k < nunits_stage; ++k) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int m = 0; m < nmt; ++m) {
                const char * const xr[2] = {
                    xs + (MMQ_FEW_NROWS*m + threadIdx.x/4 + 0)*sram_stride + (off_row[2*m + 0] + off_stage) % 16 + k*unit_size,
                    xs + (MMQ_FEW_NROWS*m + threadIdx.x/4 + 8)*sram_stride + (off_row[2*m + 1] + off_stage) % 16 + k*unit_size,
                };
                mmq_few_vec_dot<type, ncg>(xr, yf[k], sum[m]);
            }
            if (has_next) {
                load_y(yf[k], iu0 + iu_step + k);
            }
        }

        ggml_cuda_syncwarp(); // the stage is overwritten in the next iteration
    }
    cp_async_wait_all();

    ggml_cuda_pdl_lc();

    // reduce the sums over the warps as one flat array
    if (ksplit) {
        constexpr int nsum = nmt*ncg*4;
        float * sum_flat = &sum[0][0][0];

        float * buf_sum = (float *) data_mmq_few;
        __syncthreads();
        if (threadIdx.y > 0) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < nsum; ++i) {
                buf_sum[((threadIdx.y - 1)*nsum + i)*warp_size + threadIdx.x] = sum_flat[i];
            }
        }
        __syncthreads();
        if (threadIdx.y > 0) {
            return;
        }
#pragma unroll
        for (int w = 0; w < nwarps - 1; ++w) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int i = 0; i < nsum; ++i) {
                sum_flat[i] += buf_sum[(w*nsum + i)*warp_size + threadIdx.x];
            }
        }
    }

#pragma unroll
    for (int m = 0; m < nmt; ++m) {
#pragma unroll
        for (int c = 0; c < ncg; ++c) {
#pragma unroll
            for (int l = 0; l < 4; ++l) {
                const int i = row0 + MMQ_FEW_NROWS*m + tile<16, 8, int>::get_i(l);
                const int j = c*MMQ_FEW_NCOLS + tile<16, 8, int>::get_j(l);
                if (i < nrows_x && j < ncols) {
                    if constexpr (has_ids) {
                        const int e = pair[2 + j]; // token*n_expert_used + slot
                        dst[int64_t(e / ncols_dst)*stride_channel_dst + int64_t(e % ncols_dst)*stride_col_dst + i] = sum[m][c][l];
                    } else {
                        dst[int64_t(j)*stride_col_dst + i] = sum[m][c][l];
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
#else
    GGML_UNUSED_VARS(x, y_ptr, dst, nunits, nrows_x, ncols_dst, stride_row_x, stride_col_dst,
        channel_ratio, stride_channel_x, stride_channel_dst,
        sample_ratio, stride_sample_x, stride_sample_dst, x_is_constant, pairs);
    NO_DEVICE_CODE;
#endif // defined(CP_ASYNC_AVAILABLE)
}

// ggml_is_constant (static in ggml.c) on the tensor that a view points to
static bool mmq_few_is_constant(const ggml_tensor * src0) {
    const ggml_tensor * x_root = src0->view_src ? src0->view_src : src0;
    return x_root->buffer && ggml_backend_buffer_get_usage(x_root->buffer) == GGML_BACKEND_BUFFER_USAGE_WEIGHTS &&
        !(x_root->flags & GGML_TENSOR_FLAG_PARAM);
}

// MUL_MAT_ID: grid y is the pairs, the kernel takes the expert from the pair and does not use the channel and sample ratios.
template <ggml_type type, int ncg, int nmt, bool has_ids = false>
static void launch_mul_mat_q_few(const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, ggml_tensor * dst,
        const int4 * src1_q, cudaStream_t stream, const int * pairs = nullptr, const int npairs_max = 0) {
    GGML_TENSOR_BINARY_OP_LOCALS;

    const int nunits = ne00 / QK_K;
    GGML_ASSERT(nunits % mmq_few_nunits_stage(type, ncg, nmt) == 0);

    const int warp_size     = ggml_cuda_info().devices[ggml_cuda_get_device()].warp_size;
    const int nbytes_shared = mmq_few_nbytes_shared(type, ncg, nmt);
    CUDA_SET_SHARED_MEMORY_LIMIT((mul_mat_q_few<type, ncg, nmt, has_ids>), nbytes_shared);

    const bool x_is_constant = mmq_few_is_constant(src0);

    const int  nrows_blk = (mmq_few_ksplit(has_ids, nunits) ? 1 : MMQ_FEW_NWARPS)*nmt*MMQ_FEW_NROWS;
    const dim3 block_nums((ne01 + nrows_blk - 1) / nrows_blk, has_ids ? npairs_max : ne12, ne13);
    const dim3 block_dims(warp_size, MMQ_FEW_NWARPS, 1);
    const ggml_cuda_kernel_launch_params params(block_nums, block_dims, nbytes_shared, stream);
    ggml_cuda_kernel_launch(mul_mat_q_few<type, ncg, nmt, has_ids>, params,
        (const char *) src0->data, src1_q, (float *) dst->data,
        nunits, (int) ne01, (int) ne1, (int64_t) nb01, (int) (nb1/sizeof(float)),
        (int) (ne12/ne02), (int64_t) nb02, (int) (nb2/sizeof(float)),
        (int) (ne13/ne03), (int64_t) nb03, (int) (nb3/sizeof(float)), x_is_constant, pairs);
}

static int mmq_few_ncg(const int64_t ncols) {
    return (ncols + MMQ_FEW_NCOLS - 1) / MMQ_FEW_NCOLS;
}

// With 2 column groups, 32-row tiles halve the src1 loads per src0 byte. With 3 column groups they spill registers.
static int mmq_few_nmt(const ggml_type type, const int ncg, const int64_t nrows_x) {
    const int nsm = ggml_cuda_info().devices[ggml_cuda_get_device()].nsm;
    return mmq_few_tall_supported(type) && ncg == 2 && nrows_x >= int64_t(nsm)*2*MMQ_FEW_NROWS ? 2 : 1;
}

template <ggml_type type>
static void launch_mul_mat_q_few_tall(const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, ggml_tensor * dst,
        const int4 * src1_q, cudaStream_t stream) {
    if constexpr (mmq_few_tall_supported(type)) {
        if (mmq_few_nmt(type, 2, src0->ne[1]) == 2) {
            launch_mul_mat_q_few<type, 2, 2>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream);
            return;
        }
    }
    launch_mul_mat_q_few<type, 2, 1>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream);
}

template <ggml_type type>
static void launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg(const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, ggml_tensor * dst,
        const int4 * src1_q, const int * pairs, const int npairs_max, const int ncg, cudaStream_t stream) {
    if (pairs) {
        launch_mul_mat_q_few<type, 1, 1, true>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream, pairs, npairs_max);
        return;
    }
    switch (ncg) {
        case 1:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few<type, 1, 1>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream);
            break;
        case 2:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_tall<type>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream);
            break;
        case 3:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few<type, 3, 1>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q, stream);
            break;
        default:
            GGML_ABORT("fatal error");
    }
}

void ggml_cuda_mul_mat_q_few(ggml_backend_cuda_context & ctx, const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, const ggml_tensor * ids, ggml_tensor * dst) {
    GGML_TENSOR_BINARY_OP_LOCALS;

    GGML_ASSERT(src1->type == GGML_TYPE_F32);
    GGML_ASSERT( dst->type == GGML_TYPE_F32);
    GGML_ASSERT(nb00 == ggml_type_size(src0->type));
    GGML_ASSERT(nb10 == sizeof(float));
    GGML_ASSERT(nb0  == sizeof(float));
    GGML_ASSERT(ne00 % QK_K == 0);

    cudaStream_t stream = ctx.stream();

    const int nunits = ne00 / QK_K;
    const int ncg    = ids ? 1 : mmq_few_ncg(ne11);

    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<int>  pairs(ctx.pool());
    ggml_cuda_pool_alloc<int4> src1_q(ctx.pool());
    int npairs_max = 0;
    if (ids) {
        GGML_ASSERT(ids->type == GGML_TYPE_I32);
        GGML_ASSERT(ids->nb[0] == ggml_type_size(ids->type));
        GGML_ASSERT(ne1 == ids->ne[0] && ne13 == 1 && ne3 == 1);

        const int64_t n_expert_used = ids->ne[0];
        const int64_t n_tokens      = ids->ne[1];
        const int64_t nentries      = n_tokens*n_expert_used;
        const int64_t nused         = std::min(ne02, nentries);
        // an expert with c > 0 entries (at most one per token) has ceil(c/MMQ_FEW_NCOLS) pairs
        npairs_max = (int) std::min(nused + (nentries - nused)/MMQ_FEW_NCOLS, ne02*((n_tokens + MMQ_FEW_NCOLS - 1) / MMQ_FEW_NCOLS));

        pairs.alloc(int64_t(npairs_max)*MMQ_FEW_PAIR_INTS);
        src1_q.alloc(int64_t(npairs_max)*nunits*MMQ_FEW_Q8_1_INT4);
        quantize_mmq_few_id_q8_1_cuda((const float *) src1->data, (const int32_t *) ids->data, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), mmq_few_ds_layout(src0->type),
            ne00, nb11/sizeof(float), nb12/sizeof(float), ne11, ne02, n_expert_used, n_tokens, ids->nb[1]/sizeof(int32_t), npairs_max, stream);
    } else {
        GGML_ASSERT(ne11 <= MMQ_FEW_MAX_NCG*MMQ_FEW_NCOLS);
        GGML_ASSERT(ne12 % ne02 == 0 && ne13 % ne03 == 0);

        src1_q.alloc(int64_t(ne12)*ne13*nunits*ncg*MMQ_FEW_Q8_1_INT4);
        quantize_mmq_few_q8_1_cuda((const float *) src1->data, src1_q.get(), mmq_few_ds_layout(src0->type), ne10, nb11/sizeof(float), nb12/sizeof(float), nb13/sizeof(float),
            ne11, ne12, ne13, stream);
    }

    switch (src0->type) {
        case GGML_TYPE_Q8_0:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_Q8_0>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q3_K:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_Q3_K>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q4_K:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_Q4_K>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q5_K:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_Q5_K>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q6_K:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_Q6_K>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ4_NL:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_IQ4_NL>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS:
            launch_mul_mat_q_few_switch_ncg<GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS>(src0, src1, dst, src1_q.get(), pairs.get(), npairs_max, ncg, stream);
            break;
        default:
            GGML_ABORT("unsupported type for few-row MMQ");
    }
}

// Min. number of src1 columns (MUL_MAT_ID: tokens) for the few-row MMQ, 0 if it is not used for this type, GPU and row length.
static int ggml_cuda_mmq_few_min_ncols(const ggml_type type, const int cc, const int64_t ne00, const bool has_ids) {
#ifdef GGML_CUDA_FORCE_CUBLAS
    return 0;
#endif // GGML_CUDA_FORCE_CUBLAS

    // NVIDIA Ampere to Blackwell
    if (!ampere_mma_available(cc) || cc >= GGML_CUDA_CC_RUBIN) {
        return 0;
    }
    // on DGX Spark the kernel needs its L2 prefetch, which is only in the DGX Spark device code
    if (cc == GGML_CUDA_CC_GB10 && ggml_cuda_highest_compiled_arch(cc) != GGML_CUDA_CC_GB10) {
        return 0;
    }
    // with fewer than 8 units of 256 values per row the warps idle, MMVQ and MMQ are better there (MUL_MAT_ID splits the rows instead)
    if (mmq_few_unit_size(type) == 0 || (!has_ids && ne00 < 8*QK_K)) {
        return 0;
    }
    if (cc == GGML_CUDA_CC_GB10) { // tuned on DGX Spark GB10, also for MUL_MAT_ID
        return type == GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS || type == GGML_TYPE_IQ4_NL ? 8 : 2;
    }
    if (has_ids) { // after MMVQ, which is faster up to its max. batch size
        return get_mmvq_mmid_max_batch(type, cc) + 1;
    }
    switch (type) { // all GPUs except DGX Spark, tuned on RTX 5090
        case GGML_TYPE_Q3_K:   return 3;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q5_K:   return 3;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q4_K:   return 4;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ4_NL: return 4;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q6_K:   return 4;
        case GGML_TYPE_Q8_0:   return 5;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ3_S:  return 5;
        case GGML_TYPE_IQ4_XS: return 6;
        default:               return 0;
    }
}

bool ggml_cuda_should_use_mmq_few(const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, const ggml_tensor * ids, const int cc) {
    const int64_t ncols     = ids ? ids->ne[1] : src1->ne[1];
    const int     min_ncols = ggml_cuda_mmq_few_min_ncols(src0->type, cc, src0->ne[0], ids != nullptr);
    if (min_ncols == 0 || ncols < min_ncols || ncols > MMQ_FEW_MAX_NCG*MMQ_FEW_NCOLS) {
        return false;
    }
    // MUL_MAT_ID: the pairs are built in shared memory
    if (ids && (2*src0->ne[2] + 1)*sizeof(int) > ggml_cuda_info().devices[ggml_cuda_get_device()].smpb) {
        return false;
    }
    // the row must hold a whole number of ring stages of the launched kernel
    const int ncg          = ids ? 1 : mmq_few_ncg(ncols);
    const int nmt          = ids ? 1 : mmq_few_nmt(src0->type, ncg, src0->ne[1]);
    const int nunits_stage = mmq_few_nunits_stage(src0->type, ncg, nmt);
    if (src0->ne[0] % (nunits_stage*QK_K) != 0) {
        return false;
    }
    // unaligned data needs the alignment of the decoder loads
    const int x_alignment = mmq_few_unaligned(src0->type, nunits_stage) ? (ggml_type_size(src0->type) % 4 == 0 ? 4 : 2) : 16;
    return ggml_cuda_is_aligned(src0, x_alignment) && ggml_cuda_is_aligned(src1, 16);
}

#else // !defined(GGML_USE_HIP) && !defined(GGML_USE_MUSA)

void ggml_cuda_mul_mat_q_few(ggml_backend_cuda_context & ctx, const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, const ggml_tensor * ids, ggml_tensor * dst) {
    GGML_UNUSED_VARS(ctx, src0, src1, ids, dst);
    GGML_ABORT("few-row MMQ is not supported on HIP or MUSA");
}

bool ggml_cuda_should_use_mmq_few(const ggml_tensor * src0, const ggml_tensor * src1, const ggml_tensor * ids, const int cc) {
    GGML_UNUSED_VARS(src0, src1, ids, cc);
    return false;
}

#endif // !defined(GGML_USE_HIP) && !defined(GGML_USE_MUSA)


#include <math.h>
#include <string.h>

#include "hvx-exp.h"
#include "hvx-utils.h"
#include "hmx-fa-kernels.h"
#include "hmx-queue.h"
#include "hmx-utils.h"

#define GGML_COMMON_DECL_C
#include "ggml-common.h"
#include "htp-ctx.h"
#include "htp-ops.h"
#include "htp-tensor.h"
#include "ssm-scan-ops.h"

struct htp_ssm_scan_hmx_thread_state {
    uint32_t                 unit_start;
    uint32_t                 unit_end;
    uint32_t                 total_steps;
    uint32_t                 pref_u;
    uint32_t                 pref_u_idx;
    uint32_t                 pref_c;
    uint32_t                 cur_u;
    uint32_t                 cur_c;
    uint32_t                 unit_q;
    uint32_t                 unit_group;
    uint32_t                 unit_head;
    uint32_t                 unit_row0;
    uint32_t                 unit_nrows;
    dma_addr_t               state_out_base;
    dma_addr_t               y_base;
    uint8_t *                y_work;
    float *                  state_out;
    float *                  state_work;
    const float *            A_work;
    float *                  B_work;
    float *                  C_work;
    uint8_t *                x_work;
    const float *            dt_work;
};

struct htp_ssm_scan_context {
    struct htp_ops_context *                  octx;
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_kernel_params * kparams;
    struct htp_ssm_scan_vtcm_layout           layout;
    uint32_t                                  unit_start;
    uint32_t                                  nunits;
    uint32_t                                  units_per_hvx;
    uint32_t                                  cur_step;
    struct htp_ssm_scan_hmx_thread_state      thr[HTP_MAX_NHVX];
};

struct htp_ssm_scan_unit {
    uint32_t q;
    uint32_t group;
    uint32_t head;
    uint32_t row0;
    uint32_t nrows;
};

static inline HVX_Vector ssm_scan_vec_softplus_f32(HVX_Vector vx) {
    const HVX_Vector     v20 = hvx_vec_splat_f32(20.0f);
    const HVX_Vector     v1  = hvx_vec_splat_f32(1.0f);
    const HVX_VectorPred gt  = Q6_Q_vcmp_gt_VsfVsf(vx, v20);
    const HVX_Vector     sp  = hvx_vec_log_f32(hvx_vec_add_f32_f32(v1, hvx_vec_exp_f32(vx)));
    return Q6_V_vmux_QVV(gt, vx, sp);
}

static inline struct htp_ssm_scan_unit ssm_scan_decode_unit(
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_kernel_params * kparams,
    uint32_t unit_index
) {
    struct htp_ssm_scan_unit unit;
    unit.q              = fastdiv(unit_index, &kparams->div_units_per_seq);
    uint32_t unit_seq   = fastmodulo(unit_index, kparams->units_per_seq, &kparams->div_units_per_seq);
    unit.group          = fastdiv(unit_seq, &kparams->div_group_units);
    uint32_t unit_group = fastmodulo(unit_seq, kparams->group_units, &kparams->div_group_units);
    uint32_t head_group = fastdiv(unit_group, &kparams->div_n_row_blocks);
    uint32_t row_block  = fastmodulo(unit_group, kparams->n_row_blocks, &kparams->div_n_row_blocks);
    unit.head           = unit.group * kparams->heads_per_group + head_group;
    unit.row0           = row_block * kparams->row_tile;
    unit.nrows          = MIN(kparams->row_tile, kparams->head_dim - unit.row0);
    return unit;
}

// Fast 4-row step for Mamba-2 (n_a == 1, d_state % VLEN_FP32 == 0).
// state_in and state_out may alias (in-place prefill update).
static inline void ssm_scan_step4(
    const float * state_in,
    float * state_out,
    size_t state_stride_elems,
    const float * restrict B,
    const float * restrict C,
    HVX_Vector vdA,
    HVX_Vector vx,
    uint32_t d_state,
    float * restrict y
) {
    HVX_Vector acc0    = Q6_V_vzero();
    HVX_Vector acc1    = Q6_V_vzero();
    HVX_Vector acc2    = Q6_V_vzero();
    HVX_Vector acc3    = Q6_V_vzero();
    HVX_Vector vx0     = hvx_vec_repl_f32(vx);
    HVX_Vector vx1     = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, sizeof(float)));
    HVX_Vector vx2     = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, 2 * sizeof(float)));
    HVX_Vector vx3     = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, 3 * sizeof(float)));
    const float * in0  = state_in;
    const float * in1  = in0 + state_stride_elems;
    const float * in2  = in1 + state_stride_elems;
    const float * in3  = in2 + state_stride_elems;
    float *       out0 = state_out;
    float *       out1 = out0 + state_stride_elems;
    float *       out2 = out1 + state_stride_elems;
    float *       out3 = out2 + state_stride_elems;

    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < d_state; i += VLEN_FP32) {
        HVX_Vector b  = hvx_vmem(B + i);
        HVX_Vector c  = hvx_vmem(C + i);
        HVX_Vector s0 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(hvx_vmem(in0 + i), vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b, vx0)));
        HVX_Vector s1 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(hvx_vmem(in1 + i), vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b, vx1)));
        HVX_Vector s2 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(hvx_vmem(in2 + i), vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b, vx2)));
        HVX_Vector s3 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(hvx_vmem(in3 + i), vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b, vx3)));
        hvx_vmem(out0 + i) = s0;
        hvx_vmem(out1 + i) = s1;
        hvx_vmem(out2 + i) = s2;
        hvx_vmem(out3 + i) = s3;
        acc0 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc0, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0, c));
        acc1 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc1, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1, c));
        acc2 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc2, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2, c));
        acc3 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc3, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3, c));
    }

    HVX_Vector_x4 acc = { .v = {
        Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc0),
        Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc1),
        Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc2),
        Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc3),
    } };
    hvx_vec_store_u(y, 4 * sizeof(float), hvx_vec_reduce_sum_f32x4(acc));
}

// Prefill 4-row step across nt tokens. For d_state == 128, keeps all 4 state rows
// (16 HVX vectors) in registers across the token chunk to avoid per-token VTCM traffic.
// Assumes x_work has already been scaled by softplus(dt) per token.
static __attribute__((noinline)) void ssm_scan_prefill_step4(
    const float * state_in,
    float * state_out,
    size_t state_stride_elems,
    const float * restrict B_work,
    const float * restrict C_work,
    const uint8_t * restrict x_work,
    uint8_t * restrict y_work,
    size_t xy_stride,
    const float * restrict dA_buf,
    uint32_t row,
    uint32_t nt,
    uint32_t d_state
) {
    const float * in0  = state_in  + (size_t) (row + 0) * state_stride_elems;
    const float * in1  = state_in  + (size_t) (row + 1) * state_stride_elems;
    const float * in2  = state_in  + (size_t) (row + 2) * state_stride_elems;
    const float * in3  = state_in  + (size_t) (row + 3) * state_stride_elems;
    float *       out0 = state_out + (size_t) (row + 0) * state_stride_elems;
    float *       out1 = state_out + (size_t) (row + 1) * state_stride_elems;
    float *       out2 = state_out + (size_t) (row + 2) * state_stride_elems;
    float *       out3 = state_out + (size_t) (row + 3) * state_stride_elems;
    const uint32_t row_vec = row & ~(VLEN_FP32 - 1);
    const uint32_t row_rot = (row & (VLEN_FP32 - 1)) * sizeof(float);

    if (d_state == 4 * VLEN_FP32) {
        HVX_Vector s0_0 = hvx_vmem(in0 + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s0_1 = hvx_vmem(in0 + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s0_2 = hvx_vmem(in0 + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s0_3 = hvx_vmem(in0 + 3 * VLEN_FP32);

        HVX_Vector s1_0 = hvx_vmem(in1 + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s1_1 = hvx_vmem(in1 + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s1_2 = hvx_vmem(in1 + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s1_3 = hvx_vmem(in1 + 3 * VLEN_FP32);

        HVX_Vector s2_0 = hvx_vmem(in2 + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s2_1 = hvx_vmem(in2 + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s2_2 = hvx_vmem(in2 + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s2_3 = hvx_vmem(in2 + 3 * VLEN_FP32);

        HVX_Vector s3_0 = hvx_vmem(in3 + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s3_1 = hvx_vmem(in3 + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s3_2 = hvx_vmem(in3 + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
        HVX_Vector s3_3 = hvx_vmem(in3 + 3 * VLEN_FP32);

        for (uint32_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
            const float * B_t = B_work + (size_t) t * state_stride_elems;
            const float * C_t = C_work + (size_t) t * state_stride_elems;
            const float * x_t = (const float *) (x_work + (size_t) t * xy_stride);
            float *       y_t = (float *)       (y_work + (size_t) t * xy_stride) + row;

            const HVX_Vector vdA = hvx_vec_splat_f32(dA_buf[t]);
            const HVX_Vector vx  = Q6_V_vror_VR(hvx_vmem(x_t + row_vec), row_rot);
            const HVX_Vector vx0 = hvx_vec_repl_f32(vx);
            const HVX_Vector vx1 = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, sizeof(float)));
            const HVX_Vector vx2 = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, 2 * sizeof(float)));
            const HVX_Vector vx3 = hvx_vec_repl_f32(Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, 3 * sizeof(float)));

            HVX_Vector b0   = hvx_vmem(B_t + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
            HVX_Vector c0   = hvx_vmem(C_t + 0 * VLEN_FP32);
            s0_0            = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_0, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b0, vx0)));
            HVX_Vector acc0 = Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_0, c0);
            s1_0            = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_0, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b0, vx1)));
            HVX_Vector acc1 = Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_0, c0);
            s2_0            = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_0, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b0, vx2)));
            HVX_Vector acc2 = Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_0, c0);
            s3_0            = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_0, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b0, vx3)));
            HVX_Vector acc3 = Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_0, c0);

            HVX_Vector b1 = hvx_vmem(B_t + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
            HVX_Vector c1 = hvx_vmem(C_t + 1 * VLEN_FP32);
            s0_1 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_1, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b1, vx0)));
            acc0 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc0, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_1, c1));
            s1_1 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_1, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b1, vx1)));
            acc1 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc1, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_1, c1));
            s2_1 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_1, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b1, vx2)));
            acc2 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc2, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_1, c1));
            s3_1 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_1, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b1, vx3)));
            acc3 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc3, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_1, c1));

            HVX_Vector b2 = hvx_vmem(B_t + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
            HVX_Vector c2 = hvx_vmem(C_t + 2 * VLEN_FP32);
            s0_2 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_2, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b2, vx0)));
            acc0 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc0, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_2, c2));
            s1_2 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_2, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b2, vx1)));
            acc1 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc1, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_2, c2));
            s2_2 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_2, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b2, vx2)));
            acc2 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc2, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_2, c2));
            s3_2 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_2, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b2, vx3)));
            acc3 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc3, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_2, c2));

            HVX_Vector b3 = hvx_vmem(B_t + 3 * VLEN_FP32);
            HVX_Vector c3 = hvx_vmem(C_t + 3 * VLEN_FP32);
            s0_3 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_3, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b3, vx0)));
            acc0 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc0, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s0_3, c3));
            s1_3 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_3, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b3, vx1)));
            acc1 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc1, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s1_3, c3));
            s2_3 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_3, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b3, vx2)));
            acc2 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc2, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s2_3, c3));
            s3_3 = Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_3, vdA), Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(b3, vx3)));
            acc3 = Q6_Vqf32_vadd_Vqf32Vqf32(acc3, Q6_Vqf32_vmpy_VsfVsf(s3_3, c3));

            HVX_Vector_x4 acc = { .v = {
                Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc0),
                Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc1),
                Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc2),
                Q6_Vsf_equals_Vqf32(acc3),
            } };
            hvx_vec_store_u(y_t, 4 * sizeof(float), hvx_vec_reduce_sum_f32x4(acc));
        }

        hvx_vmem(out0 + 0 * VLEN_FP32) = s0_0;
        hvx_vmem(out0 + 1 * VLEN_FP32) = s0_1;
        hvx_vmem(out0 + 2 * VLEN_FP32) = s0_2;
        hvx_vmem(out0 + 3 * VLEN_FP32) = s0_3;

        hvx_vmem(out1 + 0 * VLEN_FP32) = s1_0;
        hvx_vmem(out1 + 1 * VLEN_FP32) = s1_1;
        hvx_vmem(out1 + 2 * VLEN_FP32) = s1_2;
        hvx_vmem(out1 + 3 * VLEN_FP32) = s1_3;

        hvx_vmem(out2 + 0 * VLEN_FP32) = s2_0;
        hvx_vmem(out2 + 1 * VLEN_FP32) = s2_1;
        hvx_vmem(out2 + 2 * VLEN_FP32) = s2_2;
        hvx_vmem(out2 + 3 * VLEN_FP32) = s2_3;

        hvx_vmem(out3 + 0 * VLEN_FP32) = s3_0;
        hvx_vmem(out3 + 1 * VLEN_FP32) = s3_1;
        hvx_vmem(out3 + 2 * VLEN_FP32) = s3_2;
        hvx_vmem(out3 + 3 * VLEN_FP32) = s3_3;
        return;
    }

    for (uint32_t t = 0; t < nt; ++t) {
        const float * state_src = (t == 0) ? in0 : out0;
        const float * B_t       = B_work + (size_t) t * state_stride_elems;
        const float * C_t       = C_work + (size_t) t * state_stride_elems;
        const float * x_t       = (const float *) (x_work + (size_t) t * xy_stride);
        float *       y_t       = (float *)       (y_work + (size_t) t * xy_stride);
        const HVX_Vector vdA    = hvx_vec_splat_f32(dA_buf[t]);
        const HVX_Vector vx     = hvx_vmem(x_t + row_vec);
        ssm_scan_step4(state_src, out0, state_stride_elems, B_t, C_t, vdA,
                       Q6_V_vror_VR(vx, row_rot), d_state, y_t + row);
    }
}

// Single-row step for tail rows (nrows % 4 != 0), unaligned d_state, or vector-A (n_a == d_state).
static void ssm_scan_step1(
    const float * state_in,
    float * state_out,
    const float * restrict B,
    const float * restrict C,
    const float * restrict A_row,
    uint32_t n_a,
    float dt_softplus,
    float dA_scalar,
    HVX_Vector vx0,
    uint32_t d_state,
    float * restrict y
) {
    HVX_Vector acc        = Q6_V_vzero();
    HVX_Vector vdt        = hvx_vec_splat_f32(dt_softplus);
    HVX_Vector vdA_splat  = hvx_vec_splat_f32(dA_scalar);

    uint32_t i = 0;
    for (; i + VLEN_FP32 <= d_state; i += VLEN_FP32) {
        HVX_Vector vdA = n_a == 1 ? vdA_splat : hvx_vec_exp_f32(hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(hvx_vmem(A_row + i), vdt));
        HVX_Vector b   = hvx_vmem(B + i);
        HVX_Vector c   = hvx_vmem(C + i);
        HVX_Vector s0  = hvx_vec_add_f32_f32(hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(hvx_vmem(state_in + i), vdA), hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(b, vx0));
        hvx_vmem(state_out + i) = s0;
        acc = hvx_vec_add_f32_f32(acc, hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(s0, c));
    }

    if (i < d_state) {
        const HVX_VectorPred mask = Q6_Q_vsetq_R((d_state - i) * sizeof(float));
        const HVX_Vector     zero = Q6_V_vzero();
        HVX_Vector vdA = n_a == 1
            ? vdA_splat
            : hvx_vec_exp_f32(hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(Q6_V_vmux_QVV(mask, hvx_vmem(A_row + i), zero), vdt));
        HVX_Vector b  = Q6_V_vmux_QVV(mask, hvx_vmem(B + i), zero);
        HVX_Vector c  = Q6_V_vmux_QVV(mask, hvx_vmem(C + i), zero);
        HVX_Vector p0 = Q6_V_vmux_QVV(mask, hvx_vmem(state_in + i), zero);
        HVX_Vector s0 = hvx_vec_add_f32_f32(hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(p0, vdA), hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(b, vx0));
        hvx_vmem(state_out + i) = s0;
        acc = hvx_vec_add_f32_f32(acc, hvx_vec_mul_f32_f32(s0, c));
    }

    hvx_vec_store_u(y, sizeof(float), hvx_vec_reduce_sum_f32(acc));
}

static inline void ssm_scan_decode_push_inputs(
    dma_queue * dma_q,
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_context * scctx,
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_unit * unit,
    void * state_spad,
    void * B_spad,
    void * C_spad,
    void * x_spad,
    void * dt_spad,
    void * A_spad
) {
    const struct htp_ops_context *            octx             = scctx->octx;
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_kernel_params * kparams          = scctx->kparams;
    const struct htp_ssm_scan_vtcm_layout *   layout           = &scctx->layout;
    const struct htp_tensor *                 s                = octx->src[0];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 x                = octx->src[1];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 dt               = octx->src[2];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 A                = octx->src[3];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 B                = octx->src[4];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 C                = octx->src[5];
    const struct htp_tensor *                 ids              = octx->src[6];
    const int32_t                             id               = ((const int32_t *) (uintptr_t) ids->data)[unit->q];
    const size_t                              state_row_bytes  = (size_t) kparams->d_state * sizeof(float);
    const size_t                              state_row_stride = layout->state_row_stride;
    const size_t                              x_bytes          = (size_t) unit->nrows * sizeof(float);
    const size_t                              a_bytes          = (size_t) kparams->n_a * sizeof(float);

    const dma_addr_t state_in = s->data + (size_t) id * s->nb[3] + (size_t) unit->head * s->nb[2] + (size_t) unit->row0 * s->nb[1];
    const dma_addr_t B_in     = B->data + (size_t) unit->q * B->nb[3] + (size_t) unit->group * B->nb[1];
    const dma_addr_t C_in     = C->data + (size_t) unit->q * C->nb[3] + (size_t) unit->group * C->nb[1];
    const dma_addr_t x_in     = x->data + (size_t) unit->q * x->nb[3] + (size_t) unit->head * x->nb[1] + (size_t) unit->row0 * sizeof(float);
    const dma_addr_t dt_in    = dt->data + (size_t) unit->q * dt->nb[2] + (size_t) unit->head * sizeof(float);
    const dma_addr_t A_in     = A->data + (size_t) unit->head * A->nb[1];

    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(state_spad, state_in), state_row_stride, s->nb[1], state_row_bytes, unit->nrows);
    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(B_spad, B_in), state_row_stride, state_row_bytes, state_row_bytes, 1);
    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(C_spad, C_in), state_row_stride, state_row_bytes, state_row_bytes, 1);
    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(x_spad, x_in), x_bytes, x_bytes, x_bytes, 1);
    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(dt_spad, dt_in), sizeof(float), sizeof(float), sizeof(float), 1);
    dma_queue_push(dma_q, dma_make_data(A_spad, A_in), a_bytes, a_bytes, a_bytes, 1);
}

static void ssm_scan_thread_f32_decode(const htp_thread_t * th, unsigned int nth, unsigned int ith, void * data) {
    struct htp_ssm_scan_context *             scctx      = (struct htp_ssm_scan_context *) data;

Raúl Valdés Vivó (September 21, 1929 – November 9, 2013) was a Cuban diplomat, politician, journalist, and author. A prominent cadre of the Cuban revolutionary movement, he served as the first and only foreign ambassador accredited to the National Liberation Front of South Vietnam (Viet Cong) and the Provisional Revolutionary Government of the Republic of South Vietnam residing continuously inside the Indochinese jungle during the Vietnam War.
He later served as Head of the General Department of Foreign Relations of the Central Committee of the Communist Party of Cuba (PCC) and rector of the Ñico López Party School.


Biography


Early activism and clandestine journalism
Valdés Vivó joined the socialist youth organization of the Popular Socialist Party (PSP) in 1946. That same year, he started writing for the youth magazine Mella, which he directed clandestinely during the dictatorship of Fulgencio Batista. He contributed to the magazine Última Hora and suffered imprisonment for his revolutionary activities.
Between 1957 and 1959, he attended the International Lenin School in Moscow. Following the 1959 Cuban Revolution, he directed a national radio newscast and became deputy editor of the newspaper Hoy until it merged into Granma in 1965. He published articles in magazines including Bohemia, Verde Olivo, Cuba Socialista, and Unión.


Diplomatic mission and the "Jungle Embassy"

In 1965, he traveled to the combat zones of South Vietnam representing the Cuban Committee for Solidarity with South Vietnam. In 1966, he toured North Vietnam and areas controlled by the Pathet Lao in Laos. In 1967, he testified regarding military escalation at the Russell Tribunal in Stockholm.
Appointed ambassador to the Kingdom of Cambodia in June 1967, he was based in Phnom Penh. On March 4, 1969, he presented his credentials to the National Liberation Front and the Provisional Revolutionary Government in a small wooden cabin deep within the jungle of Tây Ninh province, near the border with Cambodia. Valdés Vivó was the sole foreign ambassador to operate an official diplomatic mission permanently based inside the liberated jungle areas.
Alongside war correspondent Marta Rojas, he published dispatches on the conflict. Later in his career, he authored documentary accounts on the secret deployment of Cuban construction and engineering instructors to build portions of the Ho Chi Minh Trail following agreements between Fidel Castro, Pham Van Dong, and General Vo Nguyen Giap in 1973.


Political and academic career
Upon returning to Cuba, he headed the Foreign Relations Department of the Central Committee of the Communist Party of Cuba, overseeing diplomatic ties with international national liberation movements in Africa, Asia, and Latin America. He subsequently served as rector of the PCC Higher School «Ñico López», leading research in sociology and political economy.


Selected bibliography

Reports from South Viet Nam (1967)
Embajada en la selva y antes: paralelo 17 (1969)
Los negros ciegos (1971)

Ethiopia: The Unknown Revolution (1978)
Reacción disociativa (1986)
Una gota en el torrente: escalinata, selva y desierto (2000)
El gran secreto del 11 de septiembre (2002)
El gran secreto: cubanos en el camino de Ho Chi Minh

Unfree Speech: The Threat to Global Democracy and Why We Must Act, Now is a 2020 political memoir by Hong Kong activist Joshua Wong, written with lawyer and columnist Jason Y. Ng and introduced by Ai Weiwei. The book combines Wong's account of his political development with letters written during imprisonment and an argument for international action in defence of democracy. It was published in the United Kingdom by WH Allen on 6 February 2020 and in the United States by Penguin Books on 18 February 2020.
In July 2020, shortly after the imposition of the Hong Kong national security law, Hong Kong public libraries made the book unavailable while reviewing titles under the legislation.


Contents

The publisher describes the book as having three parts. The first follows Wong's path from school activism to his role in the Umbrella Movement and the founding of Demosistō. The second collects letters he wrote while imprisoned, and the third presents a manifesto urging readers outside Hong Kong to defend democratic institutions and freedoms.
Wong's narrative begins with his childhood and education in Hong Kong. It describes the school-based campaign against the proposed moral and national education curriculum, the growth of Scholarism, and the protests of 2014. It then turns to the prosecution and imprisonment of activists, Wong's Christian beliefs, and his view that Hong Kong's political situation has implications beyond the territory. The final section recommends practical forms of civic participation, including following political developments, checking information and taking part in organising and election campaigns.


Reception
Reviewing the book for The Observer, Tim Adams called it "a call to arms for the Snapchat generation". He treated it as both a memoir of an unusually eventful decade and a manual for political participation. Adams found the prison-letter section less fluent in places, describing parts of the second half as having the flattened tone of a court report or legal document, but emphasised the book's insistence that democratic action begins with small acts.
In The Guardian, Julia Lovell described the book as a memoir and "fervent call to arms". She praised Wong's avoidance of individual grandstanding and his emphasis on the people of Hong Kong, while also discussing the book's sharp distinction between democratic Hong Kong and mainland China. Lovell cautioned that activists should not demonise China or its people, and said that Wong's long-term influence was difficult to assess while the political situation remained unresolved. She nevertheless concluded that his "tenacious dynamism" made the book a guide to mobilising for democracy and representation.


Library review
On 30 June 2020, China's national security legislation for Hong Kong came into force. Within days, books by Wong and other pro-democracy figures disappeared from public-library catalogues or were marked unavailable. Reuters reported that a search for Wong's books, including Unfree Speech, returned results describing them as unavailable or under review. The Leisure and Cultural Services Department said that it would review whether particular books violated the legislation and would seek legal advice; during that process, the books would not be available for borrowing or reference.
Wong characterised the development as censorship, while Hong Kong and mainland officials said that the legislation targeted a small number of offenders and would not curtail freedom of speech or the press.


Editions
The British WH Allen edition, with ISBN 978-0-7535-5479-1, was published on 6 February 2020; the US Penguin Books paperback, ISBN 978-0-14-313571-5, followed on 18 February 2020. WH Allen issued an electronic edition (ISBN 978-0-7535-5480-7) and Penguin Audio an unabridged recording narrated by Dexter Galang (ISBN 978-0-593-28588-6), both on their respective release dates. The two markets used different jackets: the British edition printed the title in cream capitals crossed out by pink strokes, while the American edition used a photograph of Wong carried through a crowd of reporters.

According to the ideals of Communism, war is only justifiable if it is waged in favour of the working people, not if its sole goal is territorial expansion or resource gain. Wars of colonialism are considered to be about "exporting" the Capitalist ideals, running counter to the Communist goals. However, bloody revolutions and civil wars, notably the Russian Revolution and the Chinese Civil War, with the objective of converting to Communism are a part of Communism.

Most militaries of Communist nations have been subjected by the Communist party of that country, with many nations' parties considering the military to be a part of the party. In the Soviet Union, the party leader was ex officio the chairman of the Council of Defence.
Despite this, many Communist nations, notably the Soviet Union, have participated in wars of expansionism and annexations. These wars were mostly to increase state power as apposed to spreading the Communist ideals, however. For example, the Winter War and Continuation War were wars between Finland and the Soviet Union in which the latter tried to annex the former in order to increase their power instead of spreading the Communist ideals.


Doctrine
According to Communism, there are only three valid reasons for war or other conflict: Defence, spreading Communism, and a Communist revolution. Any other wars, such as wars for expansion or resource gain, are considered Imperialist Wars, and unjust.
Armed forces are, according to Karl Marx, necessary for the proletariat to retain their freedom, but a traditional ranked system of the military goes against the core principals of Communism, instead having elected leadership in a national proletariat militia. This militia would be fed by conscription, and be made of the workers of the country.
A Communist revolution or Civil War is a core part of Communism, with the final line of the Communist Manifesto being a call for workers to rebel. Because of this, Communism cannot exist without some sort of conflict in order to found a Communist state, unless it wins through an electoral process.


Communist Warfare by Country


Afghanistan

The majority of the existence of the Democratic Republic of Afghanistan was marked by the Soviet-Afghan War, with it surviving only four months after the collapse of the Soviet Union.
After the Saur Revolution in 1978, the People's Democratic Party of Afghanistan took control of Afghanistan. The guard regiments of the old Afghan army were largely kept by the new DRA, and were used alongside Soviet forces against the Afghan mujahideen during the Soviet-Afghan War.


Albania

The People's Socialist Republic of Albania was a satellite state of the Soviet Union. Its military was the Albanian Peoples' Army, and was considered to be a part of the Albanian Communist Party. It was fed by conscription, and fought in three wars: World War 2, the Greek Civil War, and Operation Valuable. It was responsible for the Corfu Channel incident, when Albanian fortifications fired on several Royal Navy ships.


Bulgaria

In 1944, the right-wing dictatorship enstated by a Zveno-led coup that had been instated since 1923 was overthrown by the Soviets after Bulgaria sued for peace with the Allies, resulting in the founding of the People's Republic of Bulgaria in 1946.
The PRB joined the Warsaw Pact in 1955, and the Bulgarian People's Army participated in the Warsaw Pact invasion of Czechoslovakia in 1968.


Cambodia
The armed forces of Democratic Kampuchea, or the Khmer Rouge, was the Kampuchea Revolutionary Army, or the RAK. It was almost constantly at either near or full war.
The Khmer Rouge was formed in 1967 with a peasants' revolt, and the RAK was formed following a coup in March 1970, which led to the Cambodian Civil War fought between them and the US-backed Kingdom of Cambodia. The Khmer Rouge won the war following their capture of Phnom Penh in 1975, and established Democratic Kampuchea, despite American carpet bombing of Cambodia.
The RAK itself was fed through conscription, and had 140,000 personnel at its peak.
After the establishment of Democratic Kampuchea, the RAK carried out the purge on the people of Cambodia, in which 1.71 million people (2.1% of the population) were murdered between 1975 and 1979. The US did nothing to prevent this until Vietnam invaded Cambodia in 1979.
There had always been border skirmishes between the RAK and Vietnam, but there was no official invasion until 1979. The People's Army of Vietnam, the PAVN, quickly overtook Cambodia, entering Phnom Penh in December 1979, establishing the People's Republic of Kampuchea, or the PRK, the next day.
The armed forces of the People's Republic of Kampuchea were the Kampuchean People's Revolutionary Armed Forces, or the KPRAF, which was formed alongside the PRK in 1979. It was only semi-official, but was fed through conscription and had 172,000 personnel at its peak.


China

The military of the People's Republic of China is the People's Liberation Army, formed in 1927, during the Nanchang uprising. Its founding started the Chinese Civil War, turning it from a revolution to a real war. The PLA was originally called the Red Army, named after its Soviet counterpart, the Red Army. The Red Army grew rapidly, gaining 195,000 personally in 6 years.
It participated in the Sino-Japanese War in 1937, largely avoiding direct conflict against the Japanese and engaging in guerilla conflict. Following the Japanese surrender in 1945, the Manifesto of the Chinese People's Liberation Army was published, and the million-strong Army was renamed to the People's Liberation Army.
The PLA won the Chinese Civil War in 1949, founding the People's Republic of China. After the War, it underwent great reorganisation, including the establishment of an air force and navy. In 1965, the Central Military Commission undid the rank system until 1988, when the rank system was reintroduced. The last of the Four Modernizations of China turned the PLA into a modern military force with modern weapons.
Today, the PLA is the largest military in the world, but has had no conscription since 1949. It is one of only 9 global militaries to have nuclear weapons.


Cuba

The Republic of Cuba has had the Cuban Revolutionary Armed Forces, or the FAR, as their only defence force since 1960. However, in its earliest form, the Cuban Army was founded in 1868, during the Ten Years' War.
The Cuban Army served on the side of the allies during the First World War, and in the Battle of the Caribbean during the Second World War. Following the Cuban Revolution, Fidel Castro reorganized the military into the modern FAR.

During the Cold War, the Soviet Union supplied financial and material aid to the FAR, which the United States perceived as a threat due to Cuba's proximity to it. The rising tensions, paired with the discovery of ballistic missiles being shipped to Cuba by a U2 Spy plane, led to the Cuban Missile Crisis of October 1962, in which the world came closer then it ever has to nuclear war. The Cuban military went on full combat readiness, and the US Navy blockaded the island. The crisis was only averted when US president John F. Kennedy agreed with Nikita Krushchev to remove missiles from Turkey in exchange for the Soviet removal of the Cuban missiles.
Aside from the Cuban Missile Crisis, the FAR served in most Soviet proxy wars during the Cold War, including Operation Urgent Fury, the Ogaden War, and the Yom Kippur War.
As with most Communist militaries, the FAR is fed by conscription for men, with a 2-year service obligation.


Czechoslovakia

The Czechoslovak People's Army, or the ČSLA, was the armed forces of Czechoslovak Socialist Republic. The Czechoslovak Army was formed after Czechoslovakia declared independence from Austria-Hungary in 1918. It was dissolved in 1938, when Germany annexed Czechoslovakia, but was reformed in 1945, as a provisional organization. The was formed after the Communist Party of Czechoslovakia took over in 1948, and was largely funded by the Soviet Union; in 1951, the latter gave almost 44 million rubles in aid to Czechoslovakia so they could purchase military equipment.
The Soviet Union considered the ČSLA satisfactory. Its rejection of the Soviet Union's call for better organization in the Warsaw Pact, the ČSLA commander's attempts to democratize the military, and Alexander Dubček's rise to power in Czechoslovakia and subsequent loosening of censorship resulted in a Soviet invasion in 1968.
Around 500,00 Warsaw Pact soldiers, mostly Soviet, invaded Czechoslovakia in August 1968. The ČSLA failed to defend sufficiently well, and Dubček was deposed.


Finland

Finland has never been Communist, but the Finnish Civil War was fought between White Finland and the Finnish Socialist Workers' Republic, or the FSWR, or the Reds.
After Finland declared Independence from Russia in 1917, and, directly following that, it was split between the Reds in the south and White Finland in the north. Tensions were always high, and, in 1918, conflict broke out.
The war lasted for 3 months, and resulted in a White victory. There were many executions on both sides, with more Reds being executed then dying in battle. After the war, Finland permanently passed out of the Soviet sphere of influence, siding with Germany during World War 2 and remaining neutral during the Cold War.


Germany

The armed forces of the German Democratic Republic was the National People's Army, or the NVA. It was established on January 18 1956, and the first unit was sworn in on April 30. It originally consisted of Communist veterans of the Spanish Civil War and former Wermacht members, with the latter taking up the majority of the upper command positions. However, by the early 1960s, most of the World War 2 veterans had retired.
The NVA never participated in full-scale conflict, but acted in logistics and support roles in many conflicts. It cancelled military aid and only providing logistics support with the Warsaw Pact Invasion of Czechoslovakia, and providing medical support in the Soviet-Afghan War.
While it never participated in any active armed conflict, the NVA came into combat readiness on numerous occasions. The construction and guarding of the Berlin Wall, for example, was an NVA project, and the Cuban Missile Crisis and the 1989 protests in the DDR were participated in by the NVA.
The NVA was originally volunteer-based, but from 1962 to the end of its existence in 1989 was fed through conscription.


References

The Yarishev Formation, sometimes spelt as Yaryshiv or Yarishyvska, is a late Ediacaran aged geologic formation in Moldova and Ukraine. Deposited sometime during 556.62 - 554.50±0.26 Ma, the Yarishev Formation is predominately composed of argillite rocks. The formation is also fossiliferous in nature, containing various large discoidal macrofossils, and an abundance of microfossils.


History of research
Prior to the 1960s, knowledge on the formations and fossils within the wider East European Platform were relatively unknown, save for the formal descriptions of the Mogilev and Ushitsa Formations in 1939 by L.F. Lungersgauzen, which were then regarded by Lungersgauzen as Cambrian and Ordovician respectively. With the onset of the 1960s, various studies were undertaken in order to compile a State geological map, which would see previously described formations, and new formations, correlated to other Ediacaran formations in the wider region. This would include the formal description and naming of the Yarishev Formation.
With this, proper paleontological studies of the Yarishev Formation, and others, would finally be undertaken. The first macroscopic Ediacaran fossils in the region would be recovered from the Bernashev Member of the Yarishev Formation by A.P. Koval in 1967, in the form of Cyclomedusa plana, which would result in further detailed studies being performed.


Geology
The Yarishev Formation can be found outcropping near to the village of Mereșeuca in Ocnița District, Moldova, as well as by the Dniester River in Ukraine, where it is unconformably overlain by the argillites of the Nagoriany Formation, whilst it is underlain by the sandstones of the Mogilev Formation, and is a part of the wider Mogilev-Podolisk Group. The formation itself is primarily composed of argillites, and is split into four formally named members. The overall thickness of the formation ranges between 40.6–100.7 m (133–330 ft).


Members
The Yarishev Formation is split into three formal members, sometimes referred to as beds. These members are as follows, in ascending stratigraphic order (lowest to highest):

Lyadova Member: This member, sometimes referred to as the "Lyadova Beds", is roughly 8.1–21.7 m (27–71 ft) thick, and is primarily composed of green and brown argillites, which is intercalated with layers of siltstone and sandstone in the upper sections. There can also be found rare intercalations of bentonitic clays and tuffitic argillites. This member was previously considered to be a part of the underlying Mogilev Formation.
Bernashev Member: This member, sometimes referred to as the "Bernashivka/Bernashevka Beds", attains a thickness of 22 m (72 ft), and is composed of clayey, medium- to fine-grained sandstones, which are inter-bedded with siltstone layers in its lower section. Towards its middle, the sandstones give way to thinly-bedded, dark-gray to green mudstones, with occasional thin layers of sandstone laminae. At the top, the mudstones become coarse- to very-coarse-grained arkosic sandstone, which is occasionally gravelly. Throughout the member there can be found beds and interlayers of bentonitic clays.
Bronnitza Member: This member, sometimes referred to as the "Bronnitsa Beds", ranges between 9.0–30.0 m (29.5–98.4 ft) in thickness, and is composed predominately of distinctive tuffeceous inter-beds. Used as a regional marker horizon, these inter-beds are composed of massive, red to light-brown tuffitic argillites, which sometimes possess rare intercalated layers of red brown and brown argillites and siltstone. Towards the top of the member, argillites become the dominate rock.
Zinkov Member: This member, sometimes referred to as the "Zinkov Beds", ranges between 15.3–27.0 m (50.2–88.6 ft) in thickness, and is composed of greenish-gray, gray and dark-gray argillites, which are intercalated with layers of light-gray sandstones towards the upper sections of the member. One distinctive feature of this member are the increasing sizes and coarseness of the grains from bottom to top.


Dating
The Yarishev Formation has been dated using U-Pb dating on zircon samples collected from the Lyadova and Bernashev members. The dates recovered from these samples yielded dates of 556.62±0.26 Ma and 554.50±0.26 Ma respectively.


Paleoenvironment
The environment at the time of the Yarishev Formations deposition was likely that of a low-energy, inner-shelf environment, similar to formations found from the Nama Group. This has been primarily inferred from the presence of burrows found within the formation.


Paleobiota
The Yarishev Formation contains a sparse record of Ediacaran macrofossils, from discoidal forms such as Cyclomedusa and Tirasiana, to frondose fossils like Charniodiscus, the agglutinated fossils of Palaeopascichnus, and the jaw-like fragments of Redkinia and Hindeodella. Meanwhile, throughout the formation, there can be found an increasingly abundant record of filamteneous microfossils, which are commonly referred to as cyanobacteria, with examples such as Lyadovia and Volyniella, the former of which was described from this formation.
